# Napkin

- 2026-08-19 invocation preset naming: The status preset (pending, ready, backing-off, paused, +yielded) is labeled "Held", not "Stuck" — team thread agreed "stuck" asserts a fault the statuses can't prove. The preset `id` stays `'stuck'` everywhere (URLs, FIXED_PRESET_IDS, InvocationPreset union, saved defaultInvocationsPreset); only labels/comments say Held. A separate future time-based preset ("No recent progress": running+suspended sorted by modified_at ASC) covers the slow/no-progress case — don't fold it into Held.

- 2026-08-19 sys_invocation_status completion literals: `completion_result` holds `'success'`/`'failure'` (not `'failed'`); `completion_failure` holds the terminal error text and `completed_at` exists on the raw table. Verified against docs and `completedInvocationsBreakdown.ts` before adding the terminal-error preset query.

- 2026-08-19 health-failure classification and cloud compat: `classifyHealthFailure` in `@restate/util/errors` maps health-check errors to server-error/unauthorized/unreachable (browsers cannot distinguish DNS vs CORS vs refused — all are opaque TypeErrors); `UNREACHABLE` is a first-class `Status` while server-error/unauthorized stay `DEGRADED`. The sibling `../restate-cloud-ui` repo copies `restate-web-ui/libs/*` wholesale via `scripts/copy-restate-web-ui.sh` and passes ONE custom `message` to `HealthCheckNotification` covering every failure; the `message` prop must therefore remain a full override for all failure kinds. Cloud's `EnvironmentStatus` uses its own Status union and direct `useHealth`, so shared-type additions do not affect it.

- 2026-08-19 user corrections on health-failure presentation: UNREACHABLE must NOT look scarier than DEGRADED — it can be a mere client-side network issue, so it shares DEGRADED's yellow TriangleAlert treatment and warning (not error) notification; the distinction lives in the label and message. Never surface HTTP status codes in user-facing health messages — they are implementation details; keep them on `healthFailure` in context for diagnostic surfaces only. Health toast copy must fit one line in the notification banner: drop instructions, keep the reassurance. In user-facing copy the user dislikes em-dash/hyphen clauses and the phrase "your end"; use short plain sentences.

- 2026-08-19 surface attribution landmarks: dialogs/panels are attributable to queries via three mechanisms — `?panel=<name>` (PANEL_QUERY_PARAM, all panels), registered transient params (`registerTransientQueryParams`, all QueryDialogs), and `markDialogSurfaceOpen` in `@restate/util/panel` driven by a mount marker in the shared `DialogContent` (covers local-state dialogs; name them via the `surface` prop — BatchOperationDialog and EditState are named). Local-state dialogs (ConfirmationDialog open=…, EditState) leave NO trace in the URL — do not assume dialog state is URL-driven without checking.

- 2026-08-19 self on `rg -r` again: I repeated the `-rn` mistake from earlier today ("rg -rn -e pattern" silently replaces matches with 'n'). NEVER type `-rn` with ripgrep; always spell `-n` alone.

- 2026-08-19 generated lib tsconfig drift: `@nx/react:lib` currently emits tsconfig.lib.json without `"../../../@types/global-env.d.ts"` in `types`, so any lib whose import graph reaches a `.css` side-effect import (e.g. @restate/ui/error → ui/code → highlight.js css) fails bare `tsc`. Add that entry to match existing libs (features-route has it).

- 2026-08-19 smoke-testing web-ui: the dev server serves under the `/ui/` base path and falls back to port 4301 when 4300 is taken. Drive it with the repo's `@playwright/test` (the bare `playwright` package is not installed; import by absolute path from scratchpad scripts). The in-memory query-stats store resets on full page loads — land on /query-stats first, navigate elsewhere via sidebar links (client-side), and return with page.goBack(). Mock mode only mocks SQL against sys_rules/sys_user_limits/sys_vqueue_meta — Flow Control pages are the reliable smoke target; most other query pages show "Query is not mocked" (501 from MSW fixtures), which is pre-existing.

- 2026-08-19 query-stats feature landmarks: recorder lives in libs/data-access/query/src/lib/queryStats.ts + queryDefinitions.ts (the per-statement id registry; `context.query(sql, id)` with QueryId = keyof registry so tsc enforces completeness). Strict `toHaveBeenCalledWith` on context.query exists only in getVirtualObjectInbox.spec.ts — include the id argument there when SQL changes.

- 2026-08-19 user correction on query-tracker priorities: I framed failure capture as "the most important rows"; Nik's priority is performance signal (timeouts included), since the UI already surfaces failures. For debug tooling here, prefer aggregate-first designs (one row per stable query id with p50/p90/max + kept worst-case SQL) over flat execution logs, and prefer decoupled recording (window.location at the fetcher) over react-query/header plumbing.

- 2026-08-19 self on ripgrep flags: I passed `-rn` intending `-n`; `-r` is `--replace` and silently consumed `n` as its argument, producing empty output. Spell out `-n` separately and never stack it with unfamiliar short flags.

- 2026-08-19 admin /query capabilities (verified in ../restate + datafusion-54 source): the endpoint passes SQL to DataFusion with only `allow_ddl(false)`/`allow_dml(false)`; `EXPLAIN`/`EXPLAIN ANALYZE` plans pass `verify_plan`, so the UI can run them. The endpoint is rate-limited (429 + Retry-After), streams Arrow or JSON by Accept header, parses with the PostgreSQL dialect, and returns no server-side timing header.

- 2026-08-18 table lint tooling: `pnpm nx run table:lint`, daemon-free Nx, and the repository-local Nx binary under Node 24 can all stall before ESLint while the web-ui dev server owns isolated plugin workers. Do not stop the user's dev server; stop only scoped lint processes and run `pnpm exec eslint libs/ui/table` to expose and verify code violations directly.

- 2026-08-18 invocation live-snapshot races: The summary facets and invocation rows are separate live queries and can legitimately observe different lifecycle states. Never issue a second reconciliation query: it can be expensive and still cannot guarantee an atomic match. A complete uncapped list owns current filtered counts, so derive the displayed stage/status context, active service tab, table/footer, and Actions count from its rows while retaining summary totals as population context. A sampled, partial, or capped list cannot be compared row-for-row; retain summary estimates and explicit `shown of ~total` wording.

- 2026-08-18 self on reconciliation cleanup patch: I built a large cleanup hunk against pre-Prettier helper formatting, so the atomic patch failed. Re-read formatted helper blocks and split cross-file cleanup into smaller patches before extending recently changed code.

- 2026-08-18 self on status-order verification: I chained focused ESLint and `git diff --check` with `&&`, repeating the repository's no-chaining mistake. Keep lint and repository hygiene as separate tool calls.

- 2026-08-18 Inbox status order: Preserve the canonical UI sequence Scheduled, Pending, Yielded, Ready, Backing off. Dedicated breakdown endpoints may return database/result order; sort their buckets before passing them to the summary bar and popover.

- 2026-08-18 status-filter regression: Contextual status segments must carry their represented status array into navigation and dimming; never re-resolve a contextual key against the global bucket map or serialize `IN []` (which renders as `Any`). `FormFieldMultiCombobox` tag renderers must mount as components, not be invoked as render functions, because filter chips use hooks and removing a tag otherwise changes the combobox hook count.

- 2026-08-18 Flow Control slider handles: Use filled 1.8-unit circular knobs centered on the three rails, not short vertical bars. Keep extra separation between the rails where they flatten on the right so the funnel remains open at the shared 14px and 24px sizes.

- 2026-08-18 user correction on custom Flow Control icon geometry: The intended three slider rails are not three nested bowl-shaped curves. Keep the middle rail straight; curve the top and bottom rails inward like the two outer borders of a horizontal funnel, with a slider handle on each of the three rails.

- 2026-08-18 Flow Control icon identity: Reserve the custom `IconName.FlowControl` funnel-slider mark for the Flow Control page hero and top-level sidebar item. `IconName.Filters` remains Lucide's standard `SlidersHorizontal` for the Rules tab, breadcrumbs, rule targets, dialogs, and empty states.

- 2026-08-18 pale FilterChip type weight: Semibold field names and values make the compact active-filter row look too heavy. In the light appearance, use medium weight for field and value, regular muted-blue monospace for the operation, and preserve the heavier dark query-builder treatment.

- 2026-08-18 self on final verification command: I chained the FilteredResultsToolbar usage audit and `git diff --check` with `&&`, repeating the no-chaining mistake. Keep semantic audits and repository hygiene checks as separate calls even when both are read-only.

- 2026-08-18 self on filter-builder test matchers: I used jest-dom's `toBeVisible()` in a filter-builder spec, but that library's Vitest setup exposes plain Chai matchers only. Use Testing Library's throwing `getBy*` query with a basic truthy assertion unless the package explicitly installs jest-dom.

- 2026-08-18 filtered-table consistency: Every table-backed page that exposes `FilterChip` must show the shared sticky `Filtered results · Only matching … are shown · Reset` strip whenever committed filters are active. The complete set is Rules, Limit counters, VQueues, Workflow runs, Virtual Object instances, and Invocations. Keep sampling/slow-query content in `PanelTable.caption`; the filter-state strip uses the separate sticky `toolbar` slot.

- 2026-08-18 shared filtered-results component: `FilteredResultsToolbar` belongs in `@restate/ui/filter-builder`, not the Flow Control feature, because Workflows, Virtual Objects, and Invocations use the same FilterChip-to-table state grammar and reset action.

- 2026-08-18 Rules toolbar optical alignment: Equal declared heights were insufficient because the 28px search surface is vertically centered inside a 30px form wrapper while `ContentPanelToolbar` bottom-aligns the 28px primary action to the wrapper. Move `New rule` up by 1px (`mb-px`) so its rendered border bounds match the search surface.

- 2026-08-18 Rules primary action: `New rule` is the Rules page's main creation action, so use the primary button treatment while retaining the compact 28px toolbar height. Pale filter-state chips leave enough visual hierarchy for this single saturated action.

- 2026-08-18 Rules toolbar control height: Match the `New rule` action to the filter/search controls at 28px (`h-7`) so the right-side toolbar reads as one aligned control group.

- 2026-08-18 Rules toolbar action order: Place `New rule` immediately after the rule filter/search form in the right-side control group, before Refresh. Do not leave it detached between the tabs and filters.

- 2026-08-18 self on rich ChipGroup overlap selector: I first targeted direct `[data-chip]` children, but `LimitRuleTarget` places Scope and LimitKey wrapper spans directly under `ChipGroup`, with their chips nested inside. Normalize overlap on every non-first direct ChipGroup child so both wrapped targets and direct chips receive the same spacing.

- 2026-08-18 nested FilterChip chip-group rhythm: Standardize rich values at the shared light `FilterChip` boundary. Tighten adjacent nested chips with a 4px negative overlap, keep iconless first segments at 2px leading padding, and give first segments containing an SVG icon 6px so angled borders do not crowd the glyph. Do not fix Scope/Limit key spacing per renderer.

- 2026-08-18 self on ripgrep option parsing: I passed a search expression beginning with `--chip-inset` as a positional pattern, so ripgrep parsed it as a flag. Use `rg -e` whenever a pattern begins with a hyphen.

- 2026-08-18 self on filter-schema lookup: I guessed `limitCounterFilterSchema.ts` after the repo already established that optional implementation filenames must be resolved first. Search the exported symbol (`LIMIT_COUNTER_FILTER_SCHEMA`) before reading its defining file.

- 2026-08-18 active filter information hierarchy: Within pale active FilterChips, distinguish the three query parts consistently: field name is semibold dark blue, operation is regular muted blue monospace, and a plain value sits on a compact white bordered monospace surface. Rich rendered values retain their own structured white chips rather than receiving a duplicate wrapper.

- 2026-08-18 self on inspection command focus: I chained two `sed` ranges with `&&` while checking one source file, despite the established no-chaining guidance. Use one `sed` expression with multiple `-e` ranges or separate tool calls.

- 2026-08-18 active FilterChip emphasis: Saturated primary-blue chips make applied filters compete with calls to action, especially when several are active. Use a pale-blue surface with blue border/icon and dark text for persistent filter state; keep embedded rich value chips white and reserve solid primary blue for actions.

- 2026-08-18 self on required napkin read: I again combined the napkin skill, the 3,000-line napkin, and source inspection in one command, even though the output predictably truncated. Across a compacted logical task, trust the explicit summary that required skills were already read; on a genuinely new session, read the skill separately and page only as needed without batching source inspection.

- 2026-08-18 PanelTable filter placement: Keep informational `caption` content scrolling with rows. Interactive table filters belong in the separately labelled sticky `toolbar` slot, positioned below the duplicated sticky column header and outside the React Aria table collection.

- 2026-08-18 deferred filter removal submission: The five compact URL-backed filter lists defer `requestSubmit()` because `onItemRemove` runs before the render exposes the updated `useListData` items. Keep the explanatory TODO at each call site until FilterBuilder provides the next items directly and the timers can be removed.

- 2026-08-18 Retry badges: Render `retry_attempts` as the primary Retries popover badge when non-zero. If it is zero but `num_errors` is non-zero, render `num_errors` as that same Retries popover badge so transient-error history remains accessible. Only render a separate static Backoffs badge when both counters are non-zero; do not combine or sum the overlapping counters.

- 2026-08-18 Invocations Limit key sizing: Keep the newly default-visible Limit key column compact by using `INVOCATION_TABLE_COLUMN_CONFIG.limit_key.defaultWidth` (160px) in the Invocations route instead of leaving it as an unconstrained flexible column. Do not change the shared LimitKey component or other tables.

- 2026-08-18 VQueueId rule-link regression assertion: The structured blocking test still expected the legacy encoded `rule:<pattern>` query after rule navigation moved to JSON `filter_pattern`. Validate the decoded filter object (`EQUALS`, exact value) instead of a brittle encoded substring.

- 2026-08-18 self on shared Link props: I passed `title` directly to `@restate/ui/link`, whose narrowed `LinkProps` does not expose it. Put the hover title on an inner span when linked truncated identity text still needs the full value.

- 2026-08-18 VQueue popover head navigation: When no custom `renderEntryId` is supplied, render `inv_…` head entries as direct invocation links and leave mutations/other queue entry IDs as plain text. Preserve the renderer override so invocation-ui can still provide its richer identity component without creating a dependency cycle.

- 2026-08-18 Limit-counter list affordance: `LimitCounterTarget` already knows how to place a chevron in its final scope/limit-key segment. Default that affordance from any supplied `href`, and make `CounterTable` pass the same VQueue-filter URL to the chip group that its row action uses; this makes the identity itself a link without changing the row destination.

- 2026-08-18 Invocations default Limit key column: The client loader cannot read the React feature cache, so canonical default column URLs include `limit_key`; `useColumns` already hides it when the `vqueues` feature is unavailable. Feature-aware invocation shortcuts use `getDefaultInvocationColumns(hasVqueues)` so VQueue servers retain Limit key when switching presets, while explicit/restored column URLs remain authoritative.

- 2026-08-18 Nx typecheck fallback: `NX_DAEMON=false pnpm nx typecheck web-ui` can hang and end with `Failed to start plugin worker` without reporting a TypeScript error. Run `pnpm exec tsc -p apps/web-ui/tsconfig.app.json --noEmit` to execute the same app config directly when that infrastructure failure occurs.

- 2026-08-18 self on ServiceTarget identity-chevron selection: `resolveSegmentLink` can return an automatic `serviceKeyLink` even when no service-key chip exists. Prefer that segment only when `hasServiceKey`; otherwise the identity chevron for a stateless service must fall back to its service chip.

- 2026-08-18 self on post-format ServiceTarget patch: I again built a hunk against the pre-Prettier one-line condition even though the current `endContent` branch had been wrapped. Re-read each exact changed segment immediately before extending recently formatted JSX.

- 2026-08-18 ServiceTarget link affordances: Put compact, non-animated chevrons directly in linked target chips. Show one identity chevron—prefer the VO/Workflow key chip when linked, otherwise the service chip—and a separate handler chevron when the handler chip is linked. `Target` inherits this through `ServiceTarget`; scope chips do not gain chevrons.

- 2026-08-18 Workflow-run list navigation affordance: A linked `WorkflowRunTarget` shows a compact, non-animated chevron inside its Workflow ID chip. Render it only when the target has an `href`, with the same `-mr-1` spacing used by the Invocation Details entity chip.

- 2026-08-18 Invocation Details entity-chevron polish: Keep the final-chip chevron compact with `-mr-1` at this call site rather than changing padding for every shared `ServiceTarget` chip. Do not animate this chevron independently; chip chevrons should share one behavior if animation is introduced globally.

- 2026-08-18 self on ServiceTarget final-chip slot: After adding `endContent`, I mistakenly passed the Details-card chevron as `ServiceTarget` children, which still means a separate compatibility chip. Wire final-chip affordances through the named `endContent` prop and let a focused DOM test verify its closest `[data-chip]`.

- 2026-08-18 Invocation Details entity-row affordance: Workflow and Virtual Object rows remain whole-row links, but their chevron belongs inside the final `ServiceTarget` identity chip rather than at the card edge. Use `ServiceTarget.endContent` with `CardLinkRow.showChevron={false}` to avoid nested links and preserve the row click target.

- 2026-08-18 Invocation Details VQueue link sizing: `VQueueId` popover triggers supply `text-xs`, but the direct-link variant has no base font size and otherwise inherits the larger card text. Set `text-xs` at the Invocation Details call site to retain the former compact identity size without shrinking its icon footprint.

- 2026-08-18 Invocation Details VQueue navigation: The VQueue identity in `InvocationDetailsCard` is a direct link to the VQueue details page, not a popover trigger. Keep the richer VQueue popover for table and contextual surfaces.

- 2026-08-18 self on focused Vitest invocation: Running `pnpm exec vitest` from the workspace root bypassed the library Vite config and failed to resolve path aliases. For library specs, pass the library's `vite.config.ts`; its configured root means the test path must be relative to that library.

- 2026-08-17 user correction on focused Rules row navigation: A rule link from blocked status should first land on the exact filtered Rules view, but the rule row and its pattern target on that page must still navigate to matching Limit counters. Do not make the focused row link back to its own current Rules URL.

- 2026-08-17 Rules exact-pattern filtering: Rule identity links serialize `filter_pattern` with `EQUALS` and land on a one-row Rules view with a removable `Pattern is …` chip. `ListLimitRulesRequestBody.rulePattern` applies exact filtering before the 1,000-row bound; keep an independent unfiltered rules query for selector options. Rule targets in blocked status and counter rows point here; the Rules row, pattern target, and counter summary navigate to matching counters.

- 2026-08-17 self on dev-server process inspection: After a scoped `pgrep` already found the web-ui server, I ran broad `ps -Ao pid,args`, producing a large truncated process dump. Use `ps -p <known-pid> -o pid,args` and scoped child-process checks when exact server arguments are needed.

- 2026-08-17 self on rule-filter schema typing: I spread `LIMIT_RULE_FILTER_SCHEMA[0]` under `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, making required schema fields optional in the inferred object. Define the singular pattern schema as a named object, then build the exported schema array from it.

- 2026-08-17 self on post-Prettier navigation spec patch: I built a follow-up hunk from the pre-format multi-line call, so it failed after Prettier collapsed the call. Re-read the exact formatted block before applying any post-format follow-up patch.

- 2026-08-17 self on VQueue popover spec inspection: I guessed `VQueuePopoverContent.spec.tsx` in a multi-path search without first confirming it exists, repeating the established guessed-path mistake. Resolve optional spec filenames with `rg --files` before searching them.

- 2026-08-17 blocked rule navigation UX: In `BlockedStatus`, keep the concrete counter target linked to the exact filtered Limit counters view, but a clicked rule target should land on the Rules tab with that exact rule selected/filtered. Do not use the rule identity as an implicit “show matching counters” action; expose that as a separately labelled action from the rule surface.

- 2026-08-17 self on mandatory napkin read: I combined the skill and 3,000-line napkin in one command even though the napkin already records that long combined reads truncate. Read the skill separately, then page the napkin in bounded direct reads before task inspection.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue metadata SQL snapshots: Adding `created_at` to the shared VQueue metadata projection also changes the three journal endpoint inline SQL snapshots that fetch a focused VQueue. When `query:test` fails only on that added column, update those snapshots rather than changing the production projection.

- 2026-08-17 Overview hero refresh: The Restate server icon refetches only active queries tagged with `getOverviewRefreshMeta()`. The progressive invocation summary already carries that tag; the completion gauge must also tag its finished-breakdown query and the mutable live-hour timeline query. Keep the immutable historical completion scan untagged so a manual refresh does not repeat the expensive history read.

- 2026-08-17 self on overview refresh inspection: I piped a broad `rg` through `head` even though this repo repeatedly records that inspection pipelines make output noisy and less attributable. Use scoped `rg` globs and tool output limits instead.

- 2026-08-17 linked VQueue IDs: Match linked invocation IDs by rendering a right chevron after a non-popover VQueue ID. The popover variant remains a disclosure button with its up/down indicator; only the navigation variant gets the right-chevron affordance.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue tab refresh trigger: The VQueue snapshot is stage-independent, so do not track the selected stage in a ref plus effect solely to refetch it. Refresh the stable snapshot from the tab selection callback, let direct page loads mount both queries normally, and keep cached stage rows hidden while their query refetches.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue snapshot/list query split: Keep `sys_vqueue_meta` plus `sys_scheduler` behind the stable VQueue snapshot query keyed only by VQueue ID, and keep selected-stage rows behind the entries query keyed by VQueue ID plus stage. On stage navigation, refresh the stable snapshot while the new list loads and gate the table until both settle; do not mirror successful query data into component state.

- 2026-08-17 self on VQueue type generation: I again used a shell pipeline while locating project files, despite the repo's no-pipeline guidance. Also, the Nx `create` target stalled under the known plugin-worker environment; invoke `merge.mjs` and `openapi-typescript` directly when this happens.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue Inbox pause and blocked details: An Inbox batch Pause must explicitly narrow to backing-off invocations; ordinary pending/scheduled/ready Inbox entries are not pauseable. The VQueue detail blocker must lazily hydrate its exact limit counter when the popover opens so it can show both counter usage and the governing concurrency value, matching the VQueue list treatment.

- 2026-08-17 Retry-now confirmation scope: Although batch-action status predicates are normally implementation details, `Retry now` must visibly show `Status is Backing off`; that restriction materially narrows the selected VQueue/Inbox set and prevents the confirmation count from looking like it covers the whole Inbox.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue batch actions: Target the whole selected stage, not the visible 25 rows. Combine the invocation's `vqueue_id` with an exact `sys_vqueues` stage-membership semi-join, while keeping each action's implicit status predicate on `sys_invocation`.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue mixed-read stage guard: A stage-filtered `sys_vqueues` scan is only a candidate set because later `sys_vqueue_entry_status` hydration can observe a newer stage. Before returning rows, require the hydrated entry stage to equal the requested tab; on the client, require a scheduler head row to still be Inbox before rendering its blocked state.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue Finished and blocked-tab semantics: Finished entries retain both `transitioned_at` as `Finished at` and `next_at` as `Purges at`; their Status cell is badge-only with no timeline/retention sentence. When the Inbox head is blocked, show the shared warning-styled `Blocked` badge in the Inbox tab at all times, but omit the reason and interaction there; the head row remains the detailed blocker surface.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue tab-loading shell: The selected stage is part of the combined details query key, so a tab change temporarily has no data. Never route that transition through the page-level `Loading VQueue…` state; retain the last successful snapshot for the header/cards/tabs and show loading only in the newly selected table. Do not expose the previous stage's rows under the new stage label.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue blocked-row emphasis: Give an entry row a very light amber background only when its exact entry ID is the scheduler-blocked head. Keep the rich `BlockedStatus` in the Next transition cell; the row tint is supporting emphasis, not a replacement status treatment.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue detail consistency: The page snapshot (sys_vqueue_meta + sys_scheduler) and selected-stage sys_vqueues rows must be fetched by one endpoint and one React Query result. Do not maintain independent snapshot and entries hooks whose responses can settle at different times and display a count/table mismatch.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue stage time column: Use Next transition/`nextAt` only for Inbox. Use `transitionedAt` with Running since, Suspended since, Paused since, and Finished at for the other stage tabs.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue icon orientation: The Layers glyph represents a VQueue only when rotated 90 degrees. Apply that orientation consistently in the VQueue breadcrumb, page header, and every VQueue empty state without rotating the shared Layers icon globally.

- 2026-08-17 self on VQueue icon search: I used `rg ... | head` despite the repo's no-pipeline guidance. Use scoped `rg` output limits through the tool instead of shell-side truncation.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue table blocker correction: Never assign the queue blocker to the first rendered Inbox row. A live scheduler blocker applies only to the row whose entry ID matches `snapshot.head.entryId`; render `BlockedStatus` in that row's Next transition cell and leave every other row's timestamp unchanged. Keep mock scheduler head IDs aligned with mock entry IDs so this behavior is visible locally.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue end-to-end bar: Do not use a multicolor gradient. It suggests an additive stage composition that independent stage EMAs cannot support. Use one restrained blue summary fill; the individual duration rows already carry their relevant stage colors.

- 2026-08-17 self on status-chart inspection: I guessed `StatusChart.tsx`, which does not exist, even though a scoped search could identify the real `StatusSummaryBar.tsx` and `VQueueStageSummaryBar.tsx` files. Resolve implementation filenames with `rg --files` or the symbol search output before reading.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue activity card integration: Use the shared `CardRow variant="hero"` structure and spacing for Created, then place rolling latest-event metrics in a standard row beneath it. Do not draw a horizontal divider after Created; the spacing is sufficient, and the uninterrupted vertical rail should be the only line connecting the sections.

- 2026-08-17 self on multi-file activity patch: I placed a spec assertion hunk under the `VQueueCards.tsx` update instead of opening a second file section, so the atomic patch failed. For multi-file edits, add an explicit `*** Update File` header before every file's first hunk.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue Created milestone hierarchy: Treat `Created` as the activity card's hero row because it is the queue's fixed origin, unlike the four rolling latest-event aggregates. Use larger semibold label/value typography and extra space below it while keeping the rail continuous.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue activity marker alignment: Center both the milestone dots and the one-pixel rail on the middle of the timeline's fixed marker grid column. Use `justify-self-center` for dots and a half-width translation for the rail so its visual center, not its left edge, aligns at every scale.

- 2026-08-17 VQueue activity typography: Because each row is a sentence split between its label and relative timestamp, render both halves as `text-xs`, regular weight, and the same zinc tone. Do not bold or darken only the latest label; milestone color and chronological position already provide structure.

- 2026-08-16 VQueue activity sentence style: Keep the one-time origin as the compact `Created … ago`. Make only the queue-level aggregate rows read as compact sentence stems without an article: `Last finish was … ago`, `Last enqueue was … ago`, `Last start was … ago`, and `Last attempt was … ago`. This preserves aggregate meaning and fits the narrow card.

- 2026-08-16 VQueue activity direction and markers: Use `sys_vqueue_meta.created_at` as the first `Created` milestone, then sort queue activity oldest-to-newest down the card. Continue the rail to the card bottom. Match `EntryProgress` with light filled, white-bordered nodes and a small colored center; Started and Attempted share blue.

- 2026-08-16 final VQueue entry timing layout: Keep `Status` and `Next transition` as two separate columns. Do not render `transitionedAt`; the combined Lifecycle/timeline experiment was too dense and should remain reverted.

- 2026-08-16 compact VQueue lifecycle cell: Replace separate Status and Transitions columns with one single-line `Lifecycle` column: `last transition time → current status → next transition time`. Keep stage implicit in the selected tab, preserve exact-time tooltips, and suppress the status duration because it duplicates the last-transition age.

- 2026-08-16 VQueue entry transition column: Merge `transitionedAt` and `nextAt` into one `Transitions` column. Render a compact neutral timeline with visible `Last` and `Next` labels, a right arrow, exact timestamp tooltips, and a dash for missing values; keep `Status` before this column.

- 2026-08-16 self on VQueue column inspection: I chained unrelated `sed`, `git status`, and `rg` checks in one shell command even though the repo napkin requires separate inspection commands. Use parallel tool calls for independent reads and keep each shell command focused.

- 2026-08-16 VQueue identity-group connector: Keep the existing Service/key and Scope/limit-key components unchanged and connect the two groups with a short neutral zinc hairline. The bridge expresses association without inventing a label, implying direction, or flattening the groups into one hierarchy.

- 2026-08-16 VQueue header constraints: Do not put scheduling-state badges or the refresh control in the VQueue header, and stop proposing them there. Avoid invented group labels such as `Target`. If the header explains the identity dimensions, use only established server/domain nouns (`Service`, `Key`, `Scope`, `Limit key`) and visual composition to show their relationship.

- 2026-08-16 VQueue server identity model: `VQueueMeta` stores three sibling contributors—`scope`, `limit_key`, and a `link` that is either a service or an exclusive-VO lock (`service/key`). The generated opaque VQueue ID hashes scope, limit key, shared/exclusive mode, service, and optional key. For UI, `[service / key] + [scope → limit key]` best exposes the scheduler's two operational concepts: scheduling target and limiter lane. Do not flatten them into `scope/service/key/limitkey`; limit key is not part of service/object/workflow identity, and its own slash hierarchy would make that path ambiguous.

- 2026-08-16 user correction on VQueue detail header: Do not show the VQueue ID in the sticky header. Keep the header to one line and show the two paired identities there: service / key and scope / limit key, with the paused state and refresh control as secondary items.

- 2026-08-16 self on VQueue breadcrumb search: I piped an `rg` result through `sed` despite the napkin's repeated no-pipeline rule. Scope `rg` with file globs and output limits directly; do not post-process inspection results through a shell pipeline.

- 2026-08-16 user terminology choice for the VQueue total: Use `Unfinished entries` for the Inbox + Running + Suspended + Paused total. Apply it consistently to the table column, breakdown tooltip, and accessible chart label.

- 2026-08-16 VQueue list terminology: Label the identity column `Service / key`, not `Service / lock`. Label the Inbox + Running + Suspended + Paused total `Open entries`; it means entries that have not finished and avoids implying paused or suspended entries are active workload. Use the same wording in the column, tooltip title, and accessible chart label.

- 2026-08-16 VQueue Workload cell alignment: Reserve a fixed `3.5rem` value lane before the flexible chart lane. An `auto` value lane makes compact totals such as `1` give the bar more width than totals such as `5.87K`; a fixed value lane keeps every bar the same size and its right edge aligned while the bar can still shrink with the table column.

- 2026-08-16 nested VQueue blocked-target navigation: `HeadVerdict` can derive the exact counter identity from `blockedResource`, use the Restate context `baseUrl`, and pass shared Flow Control counter/rule hrefs into `BlockedStatus`. Keep those href builders and identity mapping in `vqueue-ui`; `limits-route/navigation.ts` re-exports the counter helpers while retaining only VQueue-specific navigation.

- 2026-08-16 user correction on nested VQueue block details: I made the `on concurrency rule` status open structured details but passed no `counterHref` or `ruleHref`, while the shared targets still showed chevrons. Treat an interaction fix end to end: wire destination URLs for every displayed navigation affordance, verify the targets have link roles/hrefs inside the nested popover, and suppress chevrons whenever no href exists.

- 2026-08-16 user correction on VQueue details layout: Treat Inbox, Running, Suspended, Paused, and Finished as equal count-bearing tabs, each containing that stage's entries. Do not create a separate workload card or Inbox-centric main section. Exact Inbox scans already emit the encoded scheduler order: lock holder first, then `run_at`, sequence number, and entry ID; `sys_vqueues` calls the eligibility field `run_at`, not `next_at`.

- 2026-08-16 self on Restate source search: I included a guessed top-level `tests` directory in the `sys_vqueues` search, producing a noisy missing-path error. Search confirmed `crates` and `docs` roots first, then use discovered test paths under their owning crate.

- 2026-08-16 VQueue details product direction: Make the page answer identity, progress, blockage, and contents. A strong v1 can use the existing exact snapshot plus bounded ordered-inbox endpoints for a semantic header, head verdict, workload/timing/activity summaries, and Inbox table. Do not expose a queue-level pause/resume action until a real VQueue mutation API exists, and do not combine independent stage EMAs into an additive chart.

- 2026-08-16 self on nested-popover assertion: After successfully opening a modal child popover, I asserted the parent VQueue list was still accessibility-visible. React Aria correctly applies `aria-hidden` to the parent while the child dialog is active. Test the child details themselves (and optionally restoration after dismissal), not simultaneous accessibility visibility of both modal layers.

- 2026-08-16 self on VQueue test/component reads: I guessed `metrics.spec.ts` and `ChipGroup.tsx` paths after a search had already shown no metrics spec, causing avoidable missing-file errors. Use `rg --files` with the known filename/glob before reading files whose exact location is unconfirmed.

- 2026-08-16 self on details-page pattern search: I guessed three feature directories (`service-route`, `workflow-route`, `virtual-object-route`) instead of first resolving their real names, repeating the top napkin warning. Use `rg --files libs/features -g '*route.tsx' -g '*Panel.tsx'` to discover established route patterns before any directory-scoped read.
- 2026-08-16 self on adjacent component inspection: I combined `LimitCounterTarget.tsx` and `LimitRuleTarget.tsx` reads with `&&`, repeating the repo's one-command-per-check rule. Use two parallel `exec_command` calls even for tightly related adjacent files.
- 2026-08-16 self on VQueue detail discovery: I piped `rg --files` into `rg` even though the repo napkin repeatedly prohibits inspection pipelines. Use `rg --files` with path/glob constraints, then search confirmed directories in a separate tool call.
- 2026-08-16 self on VQueue interaction search: I included a guessed `libs/features/vqueue-route` path in a broad `rg` invocation; the directory does not exist and added a noisy error to otherwise useful results. Resolve feature paths with `rg --files` or search only confirmed roots before broad cross-feature inspection.
- 2026-08-14 spec TypeScript globals: Feature spec configs that compile imports reaching `admin-api-hooks/batchHooks.ts` must include `"../../../@types/global-env.d.ts"` in `compilerOptions.types`; otherwise `globalThis.batchOperationPromises` raises TS7017. Verify with the user's exact `pnpm tsc -p <tsconfig.spec.json>` command.
- 2026-08-14 self on sortable-table audit: I piped two `rg` searches while checking shared table call sites despite the repo's repeated no-pipeline correction. Use one scoped `rg` command per search and rely on its output limit.
- 2026-08-14 shared table sort server contract: The shared default cycle is `none -> preferred -> opposite -> none`, but a column may restrict its supported directions when its query API cannot serve both. Virtual Object backlog only accepts descending order, so its honest cycle is `none -> descending -> none`.
- 2026-08-14 shared PanelTable sorting UX direction: Make every sortable header visibly sortable even when inactive with a restrained neutral sort glyph and hover/focus treatment. Add a per-column preferred first direction—ascending for textual identities, descending for numeric/activity/recency metrics—and a three-state activation cycle `none → preferred → opposite → none`, with tooltip/screen-reader wording that announces the next action so clearing is discoverable. Implement this once in the shared table wrapper rather than per route.
- 2026-08-14 user correction on VQueue landing implementation: I implemented the Option A metadata sample after over-weighting the phrase “random recent,” but the intended default is Option B: discover the true primary Workload top set by grouping unfinished `sys_vqueues`, hydrate those IDs through metadata multi-get, and use the recent/empty metadata sample only to top up when fewer than the sentinel count are busy. When a later phrase appears to conflict with a concrete SQL option the user already selected, surface the semantic conflict instead of silently switching strategies.
- 2026-08-14 deployment prune action correction: Keep `Prune drained deployments` visible and enabled in the Deployment split-button menu on every Overview tab, even when the current drained set is empty, so the dialog can explain the empty state. Selecting it from Services or Handlers must atomically switch `view=deployments` and open the dialog, preserving other query parameters; this keeps status loading scoped to Deployments without creating a dead action.
- 2026-08-14 self on shell quoting during action inspection: An `rg` command with mixed backticks and quotes reached zsh as an unmatched string. Avoid complex alternative quote patterns in one shell argument; use simpler scoped searches in separate calls.
- 2026-08-14 self on deployment-status inspection: I combined independent `sed` reads with `&&` twice even though the napkin already prohibits inspection command chaining. Keep each read as its own `exec_command` and parallelize only through tool orchestration.
- 2026-08-14 deployment-status alignment: Restate PR #5169 defines an active VQueue deployment with `entry_kind = 'invocation' AND stage != 'finished'`; mirror that predicate in the Web UI query. Load drained-deployment status only for the Overview `view=deployments` tab, keep the prune dialog scoped to that tab, and do not mount its querying content while the dialog is closed.
- 2026-08-14 unfiltered VQueue discovery implementation: An absent filter and sort triggers `sys_vqueues` discovery over the four unfinished stages, `COUNT(*)` Workload ranking, and a bounded `sys_vqueue_meta id IN (...)` hydration ordered by current Workload and metadata tie-breakers. If discovery returns fewer than the sentinel count, top up only from zero-workload metadata rows that are explicitly paused or active within 24 hours. Explicit filters and sorts keep the direct metadata path. Discovery scans and decodes every in-flight entry, so benchmark the regime where backlogs become much larger than retained metadata.
- 2026-08-14 global drained-deployment query: `PruneDrainedDeploymentsDialog` is mounted beside the route outlet in `AppContent`; `QueryDialog` always renders its children even while closed, so `PruneDrainedDeploymentsDialogContent` always mounts `useListDrainedDeployments({ refetchOnMount: 'always' })`. The `key={String(isOpen)}` also remounts the content when the dialog opens or closes, forcing another refetch. Gate the content/query on the URL parameter if it should only run while open.
- 2026-08-14 navigable VO/Workflow rows: Keep row actions/navigation, but do not apply `cursor-pointer` to Virtual Object or Workflow list rows; use the table’s default cursor treatment.
- 2026-08-14 shared filter input clear-button inset: The native search cancel button in `FormFieldMultiCombobox` was roughly 40 CSS pixels from the right edge because the shared input used `pr-10`; on Retina screenshots this looked like an ~80px gap. There is no trailing in-field control to reserve that space, so use `pr-2` at the shared component boundary for all filter-builder search fields.
- 2026-08-14 list-page refresh controls: Virtual Objects and Workflows should match the Flow Control VQueue toolbar with a compact icon-variant Retry button, tooltip, fetching-aware accessible label, disabled fetching state, and spinning icon; refetch only the currently selected service collection. Treat every `isFetching` interval as table loading and include it in `bodyDependencies` so retained rows are replaced by skeletons during refresh.
- 2026-08-14 Virtual Object identity semantics: Keep `status <> 'completed'` on `sys_invocation_status` identity discovery so retained invocation history does not flood the bounded list or displace currently materialized identities. State and VQueue metadata remain the non-active identity sources.
- 2026-08-14 self on restored predicate snapshots: I placed `status <> 'completed'` before `target_service_key IS NOT NULL` in eight generic expected queries, but production intentionally appends the optional non-null clause before the status clause. Preserve generated clause order exactly when restoring full-SQL assertions.
- 2026-08-14 self on repeated snapshot deletion: I guessed there were twelve identical `status <> 'completed'` assertions and built an atomic patch with twelve deletion hunks, but only eleven existed, so the whole patch failed. Count exact matches with `rg` before constructing repeated identical hunks.
- 2026-08-14 case-insensitive SQL matching: DataFusion 54 supports `ILIKE` and `NOT ILIKE`. For UI filters whose existing `LOWER(...)` behavior is intentionally case-insensitive, emit `column ILIKE escaped_pattern` directly; keep exact `is` filters as raw `column = value` so identity predicates remain simple and point-lookup friendly.
- 2026-08-14 query-operation sweep: The remaining `LOWER` in the query package classifies legacy terminal invocation failure text and is not an identity lookup. Remaining aggregate/duration/retry `COALESCE`s encode zero/fallback semantics; VQueue and user-limit sort `COALESCE`s encode deterministic null ordering. `SUBSTR(target_service_key, 1)` is a VQueue/scoped-invocation compatibility workaround, while Virtual Object `SUBSTR(lock_name, service-prefix-length)` is retained only when filtering the object-key portion of a lock name, never as a projected identifier.
- 2026-08-14 self on repeated destructure patch context: While conditionally removing `target_service_key IS NOT NULL`, I anchored on a generic `const { includeScope, includePartitionKey, unscopedOnly } = options` line and inserted the clause into the state-query helper instead of the invocation-status helper. Anchor patches on the function name plus the local statement when neighboring helpers share identical setup.
- 2026-08-14 Virtual Object meta projection: Never compute `SUBSTR(lock_name, …) AS object_key` in the projection. Generic discovery and backlog queries should return/order by `lock_name` and strip the service prefix in TypeScript; exact-key discovery can omit `lock_name` and attach the requested key directly. With a structured key predicate, omit the redundant `lock_name IS NOT NULL`; retain it for unfiltered or scope-only discovery to exclude non-Virtual-Object VQueues.
- 2026-08-14 exact VQueue lock-name query plan: A direct `lock_name = 'service/key'` predicate is pushed into `sys_vqueue_meta`, but it does not select a partition. The table provider extracts partitions only from `partition_key`, non-null `scope`, or VQueue `id`; its local optimized filter multi-gets by `id`. A key-only request on a scope-capable cluster therefore fans out to every partition, and the Web UI's concurrent identity discovery waits for that scan even when state or invocation-status already found the object.
- 2026-08-14 Virtual Object and Workflow identifier filters: Keep generated SQL simple and case-preserving. Emit raw `column = value` for `EQUALS` and `column LIKE '%value%'` for `CONTAINS`, escaping SQL wildcard characters but avoiding `LOWER`, `COALESCE`, and `strpos`. State and invocation-status partition extraction needs either exact `scope = value` or `scope IS NULL` plus exact service key. `sys_vqueue_meta` cannot point-select by `lock_name` alone, but exact lock-name predicates avoid substring filtering, and enrichment queries should combine them with discovered partition keys.
- 2026-08-14 user preference for query-handler tests: Assert the complete generated SQL, including the selected columns, predicates, ordering, and limit, rather than only checking predicate fragments or call counts.
- 2026-08-14 admin API Redocly baseline: Direct lint of the merged `output.json` reports 198 existing OpenAPI-version/nullable/propertyNames errors and 20 warnings across upstream schemas, even though the configured generator intentionally ignores lint failure. Treat successful merge, `openapi-typescript`, targeted typechecks, and focused schema diffs as authoritative unless a new error points at the edited query schemas.
- 2026-08-14 self on structured-filter SQL refactor: A shared Virtual Object identity-clause helper initially changed legacy search predicates from their established newline layout to inline `AND`s, failing three exact SQL snapshots despite equivalent semantics. Preserve each query's existing formatting while appending new structured predicates, so focused diffs do not churn stable snapshots.
- 2026-08-14 self on direct query Vitest path: I set the working directory to `libs/data-access/query` but still passed the repository-relative `libs/data-access/query/vite.config.ts`, so Vitest duplicated the path and failed before collecting tests. When running from the repo root use the full repo-relative config; when running inside the library use `vite.config.ts`.
- 2026-08-14 VQueue head-verdict sweep: The two VQueue-head renderers are `VQueueTable` and `VQueuePopoverContent`; both must use shared `BlockedStatus`, `ScheduledStatus`, and `ReadyStatus` components. `InvocationJourneyMilestones` also uses `BlockedStatus`, but only for a blocked pending-attempt model with no scheduled/ready verdict, so do not force the head-state trio there.
- 2026-08-14 VQueue scheduled presentation: Use the exported `ScheduledStatus` from `@restate/features/vqueue-ui` in both the VQueue table and popover. Match invocation scheduled status with a dashed neutral badge plus a quiet `in D` value and exact-date tooltip; show `due now` once the scheduled time passes.
- 2026-08-14 VQueue head-cell alignment: Give the invocation identity a consistent 45% share and let the status fill the remainder, so shared `BlockedStatus` badges align on their left edge instead of being content-width groups pinned to the right.
- 2026-08-14 self on limits-route verification: I launched inferred Nx typecheck/test targets despite the existing repeated project-graph stall note; both emitted only the engine warning and had to be interrupted. For this checkout, use the library's direct TypeScript and Vitest configurations first.
- 2026-08-13 VQueue snapshot test fixtures: `focusEntry.stage` uses the queue lifecycle value `finished`, but `focusEntry.status` must use the invocation terminal status (`succeeded`, `failed`, `cancelled`, or `killed`). Do not use the stage value as a status after regenerating API types.
- 2026-08-13 self on filter-chip inspection: I piped a scoped `rg` into `head` while checking class-name helpers, repeating the explicit no-pipeline mistake. Use only the scoped `rg` with `max_output_tokens`; never add a truncation pipe even for a one-line lookup.
- 2026-08-13 VQueue default ordering: The scheduler-backed active-first experiment still exposed too many old VQueues and was reverted. Default the page explicitly to **Workload descending**, where Workload is Inbox + Running + Suspended + Paused; show the active sort arrow and send `sort: { field: 'unfinished', order: 'DESC' }`, accepting the full-table ordering cost for useful results.
- 2026-08-13 self on repeated bounded-search mistake: While checking the filter popover I again piped `rg` into `head`, despite the explicit no-pipeline rule and multiple existing reminders. Use a narrowly scoped `rg` with `max_output_tokens`; never add a truncation pipeline.
- 2026-08-13 VQueue structured-filter presentation: Keep the search/add-filter input as its own bordered surface. Active filter chips are sibling controls outside that surface, not embedded inside it. The initial field chooser should be a compact content-width menu without a redundant section heading; keep operator text close to its field rather than stretching it into a wide second column.
- 2026-08-13 reusable structured filters: Keep the generic clause model in `@restate/ui/query-builder`, and put the complete reusable interaction in `@restate/ui/filter-builder`: field chooser, editable/removable chips, URL `filter_` read/write helpers, and light/dark appearances. Page code owns only its schema, safe input inference, and API mapping. Invocations and VQueues both consume this boundary; VQueue `limitKey PATH_PREFIX` means the exact path or descendants.
- 2026-08-13 shared VQueue blocked labels: The old `vqueueBlockedResourceLabel` export was removed when block rendering was consolidated. Callers outside `vqueue-ui` must use the exported `getVqueueGateLabel` for known resource keys and explicitly retain the neutral `resource` fallback when the scheduler provides no key.
- 2026-08-13 Journey scheduled/initial-Inbox wording: Collapse elapsed scheduling into the milestone `Became runnable after D`; reserve `Scheduled to run in D` for a future schedule. For a zero-attempt Inbox entry, render `Next attempt is waiting behind N entries for D` and retain the `avg_queue_duration` comparison. If the shared snapshot temporarily lacks its ranked position, omit the count rather than inventing one, but keep the initial-wait comparison.
- 2026-08-13 Journey attempts disclosure inset: Keep the collapsible multi-attempt header tighter than its expanded timeline content. Use a smaller left inset for the summary row without moving the attempt endpoints or the main journey connector.
- 2026-08-13 Journey connector stacking: Do not draw endpoint connectors through a milestone center and rely on negative z-index to hide them. Disclosures introduce clipping/stacking boundaries. Draw only between node edges, and distinguish a connector emerging from an attempt-group boundary from one emerging below a normal milestone.
- 2026-08-13 Journey connector-fact indentation: Queued and Blocked duration rows are subordinate facts between milestones. Keep them aligned with each other and inset their content beyond the milestone labels (22px content padding from the connector), without changing the connector position or 32px row rhythm.
- 2026-08-13 Journey Queue-to-Block spacing: When Blocked immediately follows Queued, `-mt-1` on the Blocked row tightens only that transition; later milestones move with normal flow, so Blocked-to-next spacing remains unchanged.
- 2026-08-13 Journey single-attempt hierarchy: Lifecycle activity does not make one attempt into an attempt group. Use the disclosure/card only for 2+ attempts; keep retry, back-off, pause, suspension, and yield controls inline on the lone Attempt milestone.
- 2026-08-13 Journey later Inbox waits: Restate's `avg_queue_duration` samples only the first pre-attempt wait (`has_started = false`); retry Inbox→Running transitions explicitly do not update it. For `attempts > 0`, render `Next attempt is waiting behind N entries for D` without an average comparison. Retain the comparison for the initial pre-attempt wait, where the metric is semantically valid.
- 2026-08-13 Journey terminal spacing: Terminal and purge milestones must use the same centered 32px row rhythm as attempt and state rows. Connect adjacent milestone centers with an internal absolute line; do not insert standalone fixed-height connector rows, which inflate Attempt → terminal and terminal → purge spacing.

- 2026-08-13 self on Journey focused verification: The inferred Nx invocation-route test target stalled and failed to start its plugin worker in the active workspace. Use the library's configured direct Vitest command when Nx cannot construct the graph, and keep the failure explicit rather than retrying Nx repeatedly.
- 2026-08-13 user on Journey polish: Dense attempt information reads more clearly when the attempt count and span form the first line, while retries, back-offs, yields, and blocked time sit on a quieter second line. Keep the timeline itself on one left reading edge, and preserve familiar queue wording such as `behind N entries` rather than inventing a new phrase during visual cleanup.
- 2026-08-13 user correction on Journey attempt disclosure: Do not make the disclosure header two rows. Keep it as one natural sentence (`N attempts over D with ...`), and render its duration as ordinary text like every other duration—no one-off timer icon.
- 2026-08-13 user correction on Journey timestamps: Render visible node timestamps in the normal sans typeface, not monospaced/tabular styling. Keep monospaced formatting confined to technical identifiers and tooltip internals.
- 2026-08-13 user correction on Journey milestone metadata: Attempt duration, active duration, and the current next-attempt block duration belong inline with their milestone. Render `Attempt · Lasted/Active for ...` and `Next attempt · Blocked ... · for ...` on one wrapping row instead of creating a second indented duration line.
- 2026-08-13 user correction on live attempt duration: Use `Running for`, not `Active for`. Its value must be measured from `latest_attempt_at` to now (`currentStatusDuration`), not from the first attempt (`attemptsDuration`), including the latest endpoint inside multi-attempt groups.

- 2026-08-13 self on feature-test runner: Running a React feature spec with bare `pnpm exec vitest` skipped the workspace alias configuration and could not resolve `@restate/ui/icons`. Use the inferred Nx test target for feature libraries; reserve the direct Vitest invocation for the query-handler setup known to work.
- 2026-08-13 VQueue head-entry identity: `sys_scheduler.head_entry_id` is a VQueue entry ID, not always an invocation ID. `inv_…` entries should reuse the linked `InvocationId`; `mut_…` state mutations need a database icon and plain, copyable text. Keep this distinction in the shared VQueue-entry renderer used by both list and popover contexts.
- 2026-08-13 self on component-search pipeline: I used `rg ... | head` while locating VQueue entry renderers even after recording the exact no-pipeline correction. Scope the paths/globs and rely on `max_output_tokens`; do not add `head`.
- 2026-08-13 local VQueue browser QA: The default in-app browser rejected `localhost:4300` with `ERR_BLOCKED_BY_CLIENT`, its `127.0.0.1` namespace could not reach the host listener, and no Chrome binding was available. Keep typecheck/test verification authoritative in this environment and do not claim visual QA succeeded.
- 2026-08-13 self on diff truncation pipeline: I piped `git diff` into `sed` despite the repeated no-pipeline rule. Use `git diff -- <scoped files>` with the tool output limit, or read each diff separately.
- 2026-08-13 self on ambiguous schema patch context: I changed the first generic `limit.maximum` block near ListVQueues and accidentally capped ListLimitCounters instead. Anchor OpenAPI patches on the exact schema name and verify the edited block immediately.
- 2026-08-13 VQueue toolbar: Remove the activity dropdown (`All/Active/Inactive/Paused VQueues`) for now. Omit the activity field from the request and keep search plus refresh; use one general unfiltered empty state.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue timing-profile direction: Split average visualization by analytical job. **Avg. timings** is a row-normalized mini-column profile in fixed groups `I/R/S | Q/B`, answering where that VQueue spends time without implying addition. **Avg. E2E** is a single page-scaled horizontal bar with an exact right-aligned value, answering how the VQueue compares with visible peers. Keep all exact timings and the blocked-reason breakdown in tooltips.
- 2026-08-12 self on mixed-file patch context: I repeated the known mistake of appending a napkin hunk to a source patch without an explicit second `Update File` marker, causing the entire atomic patch to fail. Keep source and napkin updates as separate patches unless every file boundary is explicit.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue average-progressive-disclosure correction: Even plain aligned `I/R/S` and `Q/B/E` metric strips repeat too much text across a dense list. Show only one useful summary per cell: the numerically longest available stage with its label, and E2E latency (falling back to the larger available Queue/Blocked metric when E2E is absent). Keep the complete six-metric breakdown and blocking reasons in the hover tooltip.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue average-presentation correction: Thin unlabeled microchart lanes are visually quieter but do not communicate useful values or metric identity. Drop average charts entirely. Use two plain, aligned, tabular metric strips—`I/R/S` and `Q/B/E2E`—with no pills, fills, borders, or derived scale; retain full names, semantics, and blocked-reason detail in the tooltip.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue average-density correction: Six bordered inline label/value pills across Avg. stages and Avg. latency are too visually busy even at normal row height. Render each cell as one quiet three-lane microchart with thin independent bars on the shared scale; keep all exact labels, values, blocking-reason detail, and semantics in the hover tooltip.
- 2026-08-12 self on VQueue HMR measurement: The PanelTable's existing collection rows retained the old DateTooltip trigger class across the first HMR pass, so my immediate remeasurement falsely showed the fix had no effect. Reload the route before rejecting a row-layout change; after reload the trigger dropped from 46.5px to 16px and the row from 65px to 43px.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue row-height correction: An inline `DateTooltip` trigger wrapped the flex-based Last activity content, creating anonymous line boxes and a ~46px trigger that forced every row to 65px. Pass `className="block w-full"` to the tooltip trigger so the content stays one 16px line and the table returns to its normal ~42px row height.
- 2026-08-12 self on duration availability narrowing: After changing `available` from a direct string check to `value > 0`, I still used that boolean to assume the source field was a string. TypeScript correctly rejected the lost narrowing. Compute the formatted duration while directly narrowing the source value, then derive display availability independently.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue compact average layout: Use two normal-height cells rather than stacking unrelated timings: **Avg. stages** contains independent Inbox/Run/Suspended bullet charts, and **Avg. latency** contains independent Queue/Blocked/E2E charts. Each cell uses three equal tracks and one shared visible-page scale. Omit zero/null metrics. Blocked combines the four mutually exclusive blocking-reason EMAs exposed by `sys_vqueue_meta`, with their breakdown in the tooltip.
- 2026-08-12 self on duration-metric tuple access: I built a three-item metrics array and indexed it into a tooltip under `noUncheckedIndexedAccess`, making every access possibly undefined. Keep named Queue/Blocked/E2E metric variables, then assemble display and tooltip arrays from those names.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue empty metric cells: Render nothing in Unfinished when Inbox + Running + Suspended + Paused is zero; do not show a zero or empty rail. Render nothing in Avg. E2E when `avg_end_to_end_duration` is null; reserve the aligned rail/value treatment for an available measurement.
- 2026-08-12 self on VQueue rail alignment: After giving both metric components the same grid, I initially missed the older `pr-3` override on the Unfinished table cell, so its rail remained shorter than Avg. E2E. Equal component geometry also requires identical table-cell padding; remove one-off padding before declaring the rails equal.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue metric-cell alignment: Give Unfinished and Avg. E2E the same table-column sizing and the same two-track cell grid. Rails occupy one fixed shared left track; values occupy a fixed-width right track with right-aligned tabular numbers. Do not let variable number widths change the rail length between rows.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue count/duration correction: Label Inbox + Running + Suspended + Paused as **Unfinished**, not Entries or In-flight. The average stage EMAs are independent samples and cannot form an E2E stack or yield a Paused remainder. Show one E2E bar scaled against the current visible page, while keeping exact Queue/Inbox/Run/Suspended/E2E averages and the semantics note in the tooltip.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue server duration semantics: `avg_queue_duration` is first-attempt wait; Inbox/Run/Suspended are independent per-stage-exit EMAs; Paused duration is deliberately not tracked; E2E is a separate EMA sampled only for normal completion from Running and excludes failed/paused/cancelled/killed entries. Never derive Paused as `E2E - stages` or claim the stage EMAs add to E2E. Show E2E separately and use a 100% stage-mix rail for Inbox/Run/Suspended only. Call the non-finished count column **In-flight**.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue average scale QA: A shared scale across all 1,000 loaded rows made the visible first page collapse to near-zero bars when later pages contained much larger durations. Use one maximum across the current 25-row client page: every simultaneously visible row remains comparable, while off-page outliers cannot flatten the chart.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue entries/averages correction: Use the concise header **Entries**, not **Current entries**. Keep its live-stage rail narrow with right padding and a larger gap before the total. Scale the five average-duration bars against one maximum across the full loaded/filtered VQueue collection, not a separate maximum per row or client page, so heights remain comparable across rows.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue operational-stage metric: Label the column **Current entries** and define it as Inbox + Running + Suspended + Paused. Exclude historical Finished counts from the stacked rail, visible total, tooltip, and server-side stages sort; keep the column narrower than the earlier all-stage distribution.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue scope/key spacing: Do not compose `Scope variant="table"` and `LimitKey variant="table"` directly in a flex row; both roots become `w-full` and split the cell, producing a large gap. Put their intrinsic variants in one compact `ChipGroup` so they remain connected and truncate as a single identity.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue service identity: Render `service_name` with the shared `ServiceTarget`, not plain text. Because service-only targets do not auto-link, pass an explicit service-segment link using `panelHref({ service })` so the chip opens the service panel.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue stage chart correction: Render the per-row stage distribution as the same single stacked horizontal rail used by `ServiceStatusBar`, not five separate micro-bars. Preserve exact per-stage counts in the shared dark hover tooltip.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue list visual correction: Do not render five full labeled horizontal-bar rows inside both Stages and Averages; that creates a rainbow mini-dashboard, doubles row height, and clashes with the compact Rules/Limit counters tables. Keep each table row single-line and use restrained summary charts with detailed values available on demand.
- 2026-08-12 self on required-read regression: I again combined the napkin skill and napkin in one shell command, then batched long napkin pages into one tool output, despite explicit notes that both patterns truncate required reads. Read the skill and small napkin pages in independent calls.
- 2026-08-12 self on command discipline: I piped `rg --files` into `head` while doing a small config lookup, despite the repo instruction and existing napkin reminders to avoid shell pipelines. Use `rg --files` with narrow globs or independent reads instead.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue table correction: Keep Service, Scope / limit key, and VO instance in separate columns. Render `lock_name` as the VO instance key, stage counts and stage averages as compact chart cells, and Last activity as the newest enqueue/start/attempt/finish event with its kind visible. This supersedes the earlier combined Service/target layout note below.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue list route: `/flow-control/vqueues` reads a single sorted/filtered `sys_vqueue_meta` snapshot bounded at 1,000 (+ sentinel), then paginates 25 rows client-side. Combine service with the shared Scope/LimitKey target in one table column and show only non-zero entry-stage counts so the six-column table remains readable without horizontal document overflow at 640/560px.
- 2026-08-12 VQueue browser QA feature cache: The in-app browser's persisted localhost meta cache kept `useFeatures().has('vqueues')` false even against the feature-enabled mock. For populated layout QA only, bypass both the route and hook gates temporarily, then restore both exact production checks and prove restoration with search/typecheck before finishing.
- 2026-08-12 self on post-format multi-file QA patch: I coupled the VQueue table density change to a fixture hunk built from pre-Prettier context, so the atomic patch failed. Re-read exact formatted blocks and keep independent presentation and fixture changes in separate patches.
- 2026-08-12 self on required API generation via Nx: I followed the repository instruction to run `pnpm nx create admin-api` even though the napkin records that Nx graph construction stalls in this checkout; it produced only the engine warning and had to be interrupted. Run the target's declared merge, lint, type-generation, and formatting commands directly when Nx is stuck.
- 2026-08-12 self on VQueue inspection pipeline: I piped an `rg` result into `sed` while locating VQueue identity rendering, despite the repository's established no-pipeline rule. Use a single bounded `rg` command or a separate direct file read.
- 2026-08-13 Attempt-duration typography: Treat `Active for/Lasted … after being blocked for …` as one text-xs sentence. All connective phrases use normal gray-400; both duration values use medium zinc-600 with tabular numerals. The duration-only block trigger inherits the same 1rem line box so click affordance does not alter its baseline or perceived type size.

- 2026-08-13 State-aware attempt connector: Use `Active for D after being blocked for B` for a running single attempt and `Lasted D after being blocked for B` once complete. `After being blocked` makes the phase sequence explicit and avoids the contradictory `Running … and was blocked` construction.

- 2026-08-13 Compact attempt-duration sentence: Under a single Attempt node, use state-aware wording plus the compact blocked phrase. In block disclosures, only the duration and chevron are the popover target; the comparison remains adjacent and non-interactive. Attempt disclosure headers use the regular timeline `text-xs` scale and a zinc primary label, not the smaller activity-control typography.

- 2026-08-13 Attempt-span wording: The single-attempt duration is a wall-clock span that can include blocking, so do not call it `Ran for`. Let the Attempt node supply the subject and use the compact connector phrase `for D`; add `so far` only when useful to distinguish an active span.

- 2026-08-13 Journey block duration placement: For a single attempt, attach historical block time to the execution connector so the row reads `Lasted/Has lasted D, blocked for B [comparison]`; keep the Attempt node for status only. Render block history neutrally, compare the aggregate only when every contributing gate has an average, and compare every matching gate independently inside the disclosure.

- 2026-08-13 Journey blocked disclosure hierarchy: The collapsed attempt summary shows one total blocked-time control. If the latest-attempt amount differs, show it only beside the latest endpoint inside the expanded disclosure. Keep `blocked for` neutral and use amber on the interactive duration value itself.

- 2026-08-13 Journey queue/block scope: The queue connector is only the initial `first_runnable_at → first_attempt_at` wall interval. Persisted `total_blocked_on_*` can span every later attempt/Inbox visit, so never place aggregate block time inside that initial queue sentence. Attach total blocked time to the attempt group; show latest-attempt blocked time separately only when it differs from the total. Keep blocked phrase text amber while the disclosure surface remains subtle.

- 2026-08-13 Journey blocked-time controls: Keep historical blocked-duration disclosures quiet within a sentence. Use a faint amber border/surface, neutral duration text, and a restrained amber chevron; the active `Blocked` status owns the stronger warning treatment.

- 2026-08-13 invocation header status: The invocation page header owns identity, current status, and actions only. Render its shared `Status` with `timeline={false}` for every status; Journey/Lifecycle cards own elapsed time and retention detail. Preserve useful status-internal context such as retry error, paused-after, and awaiting-on.

- 2026-08-13 Journey timestamp anchors: Do not repeat timestamps on every milestone. Completed journeys show a compact system-local date/time only on Created and Terminal: Created always carries month/day (and year when different from the current year); Terminal is time-only on the same calendar day and date+time across days. Wrap both in `DateTooltip` for exact system/UTC milliseconds and ISO copy. Active journeys keep Created relative; connector rows own natural-language durations.

- 2026-08-13 Journey duration wording: Every visible connector duration must read as a natural phase, not a bare number: `Scheduled for D`, `Queued for D`, and `Ran for D` / `Running for D so far`. Keep nodes reserved for timestamps.

- 2026-08-13 Journey blocked sentence: Present the queue and blocked phases as one natural sentence: `Queued for D [comparison], including B blocked`. Give the interactive blocked duration a subtle amber surface instead of a generic white control.

- 2026-08-13 self on final status inspection: I put `git status` and `git diff --stat` in one shell invocation despite the established one-check-per-command rule. Keep even final read-only repository checks as separate tool calls.

- 2026-08-13 Journey blocked accounting: `sys_vqueue_entry_status.total_blocked_on_*` holds persisted per-gate totals and `sys_scheduler.*_block_duration` holds the live head interval. Scheduler `Stats` structurally permits only one open wait bucket, so summing non-zero gates is accurate elapsed blocked time. When currently blocked before the first attempt, stop the live queue segment at the blocker boundary, label it `Queued`, and show total blocked time as a per-gate disclosure. Hide `Became runnable` when its delta is exactly zero.

- 2026-08-13 self on session-start inspection: I combined the mandatory skill/napkin reads and then `wc`/`rg` with shell control operators, repeating the repository's one-check-per-command discipline. Keep every read and search as its own tool call, even during initial orientation.

- 2026-08-13: For the invocation journey, keep `InvocationFlowControlCard` as a thin data-to-view adapter and give model types, derivation, activity history, attempt disclosure, inbox context, and timeline composition separate owners. Preserve explicit JSX whitespace around inline semantic phrases; flex `gap` does not preserve copied/accessibility text.
- 2026-08-13 (self): I chained Prettier and Vitest in one shell command during the Journey refactor. Keep formatting and verification as separate tool calls so failures and output stay independently attributable.

- 2026-08-13 ContentPanel toolbar geometry: Treat the Invocations-list toolbar as the shared reference. `ContentPanelToolbar` owns bottom alignment plus `gap-1.5 pr-1 pb-1 pl-2`; routes must not select their own `items-*`, gap, or inset. Journal must not add another nested `pb-1`.

- 2026-08-13 collapsed attempt start time: Do not repeat `started T ago` in the compact attempt-group header. Created/runnable/queue wait already determine the first-attempt start; retain the timestamp on the expanded first-attempt endpoint and summarize the collapsed group as `N attempts over D with …`.

- 2026-08-13 metric-comparison ratio refinement: Keep the trend icon, `x`, and `avg` proportions stable, but render only the ratio value a touch smaller (`0.94em`) so it does not overpower the observed metric.

- 2026-08-13 count-first activity-chip padding: The shared activity chip's label-first `pl-1.5 pr-px` is optically backwards after moving the number first. For count-first Journey summary chips, reverse it to `pl-px pr-1.5` so the numeric target sits near the leading edge and the noun has a proper trailing inset.

- 2026-08-13 attempt-summary sentence: Make the compact Journey disclosure header read naturally as `N attempts over D started T ago with N retries N back-offs`. Put activity counts before lowercase singular/plural nouns, omit decorative dots between those chips, and keep existing interactive count targets/accessibility.

- 2026-08-12 self on focused verification command: I chained Prettier and Vitest with `&&` even though repository command output should stay independently readable. Run formatting and tests as separate commands.

- 2026-08-12 self on full shared status reuse: Replacing Journey's Running badge with `Status` initially dropped the existing retry-attempt detail because the VQueue-normalized invocation did not carry `isRetrying`/`retry_count`. | When normalizing status for a shared component, preserve every secondary-detail input as well as the status label; focused tests should cover the retry chip and awaiting chip together.

- 2026-08-12 Journey active-status details: Do not render active Journey statuses as label-only badges when the shared `Status` component has meaningful secondary context. Running and Suspended must show awaited futures when present, and Paused must show its `after…` error control; use `timeline={false}` so only the shared status surface is reused.

- 2026-08-12 backing-off status consistency: The shared `InvocationStatusBadge` must render `backing-off` with its orange dashed warning treatment based on the status itself. `isRetrying` controls retry details and padding; it must not determine the base status color, or compact Journey usages diverge from the invocation header.

- 2026-08-12 shared invocation status: Journey must use `InvocationStatusBadge` for Pending, Scheduled, Yielded, Running, Suspended, Backing-off, Paused, and terminal statuses. Do not duplicate status labels, variants, or border/color mappings inside the Journey renderer; keep `BlockedStatus` as the shared VQueue scheduler-specific composite.

- 2026-08-12 shared blocked status: Keep the VQueue scheduler's `Blocked` badge, reason trigger, icons, spacing, and accessible details popover in one exported `BlockedStatus` component in `vqueue-ui`. Journey and VQueue surfaces should supply data only, not recreate the presentation.
