- 2026-08-12 temporary Journey labs: once the invocation Journey is integrated and reviewed with live data, remove the `flow-control-lab` route, mock scenarios, dedicated lab test, and lab export. Keep the reusable renderer in a production-named `InvocationJourneyCard` module.

- 2026-08-12 journey queue-wait wording: the timeline position already establishes that the queue interval precedes the initial attempt. Label the historical measurement `Queue wait`, not `First queue wait`; reserve `Queueing` for a live pending interval.

- 2026-08-12 limit-counter empty-state wording: Avoid “governed” in user-facing counter copy. For the “Rule is any” empty state, use `No matching limit counters`; the description can clarify that none currently match a configured rule.
- 2026-08-12 counter ordering: Counter list SQL uses the requested field/direction first, then remaining useful defaults (`waiting DESC`, `usage DESC`, `rule pattern ASC`), and finally scope/l1/l2 identity. This makes each page deterministic and meaningfully ordered within primary-sort ties.
- 2026-08-12 playground match styling: Do not put the matching concrete-key examples inside a green container. Keep every Scope/Limit key target neutral and add the result glyph to the leading Scope chip: green check for Applies, neutral X for Doesn't apply.
- 2026-08-12 rule playground semantics: Playground examples are synthetic concrete scope/limit-key identities used to test a pattern, not observed runtime limit counters and not rules. Compose shared `Scope` + `LimitKey` chips without gauge/slider icons.
- 2026-08-12 Flow control tab freshness: Rules and Limit counters are route-backed tabs that remount their queries. Both `useListLimitRules` and `useListUserLimits` must use `staleTime: 0`, `gcTime: 0`, and `refetchOnMount: 'always'` so every tab switch issues fresh requests instead of reviving cached tab data.
- 2026-08-12 rule playground examples: In Applies/Doesn't apply examples, do not display concrete limit-counter identities as raw `scope/l1/l2` strings. Reuse `LimitCounterTarget` with `tight` density; let the row label and subtle outer tone communicate match state while the shared gauge identifies the object as a limit counter.
- 2026-08-12 table header wording: Prefer concise headers when the table context already supplies the noun; in the Rules table, use `Backlogged / total` instead of repeating `limit counters`.
- 2026-08-12 ContentPanel responsive tab breakpoint: The app sidebar becomes a mobile drawer below `md`, so shared `ContentPanel` tabs should also switch from the desktop tab list to `MobileTabsDropdown` below `md` (`md:flex` / `md:hidden`), not below `sm`.
- 2026-08-12 mobile dropdown clarification: When the user says to keep the mobile dropdown but horizontally stack its icon and text, do not reinterpret this as changing the tab mode. Keep `MobileTabsDropdown` and explicitly make both its trigger and selected-label wrapper `flex-row` so it renders `[icon][text]`.
- 2026-08-12 mobile selected-tab label: A selected tab label can be a fragment containing an icon and text. Render the shared `MobileTabsDropdown` label wrapper as an aligned flex row with a non-shrinking SVG; a plain inline wrapper lets the block-level SVG stack above the text.
- 2026-08-12 responsive rule-filter width: Do not use `shrink-0` on the Limit counters rule filter; it makes the search field collapse first. Let the filter render at its natural width up to 22rem when space exists, but flex-shrink its shared rule target down to a 7rem usable minimum under pressure.
- 2026-08-12 Flow control mobile tabs correction: Keep `ContentPanel`'s established selected-tab dropdown below `sm` for Flow control. Remove the one-off `mobileMode` API after reverting; Limit counters search remains hidden on mobile and the rule-filter compound control remains visible.
- 2026-08-12 self on selected-rule compactness: I interpreted `compact` as horizontal compression and capped the desktop filter at 16rem, causing ordinary patterns to truncate aggressively. The request was vertical breathing room only. Keep the 20px tight chip height, restore the target's native horizontal padding, and allow 22rem on desktop so the integrated X does not steal the rule's previous display width.
- 2026-08-12 selected-rule chip height: Keep the compound rule-filter surface at 28px, but use a reusable `tight` `ChipGroupDensity` with 20px chips for its selected Scope/limit-key target. This produces 3px of vertical breathing room inside the control without changing rule targets in tables or dropdown options.
- 2026-08-12 compact selected-rule filter: Make the rule filter a compound control with one shared outer border/background/shadow. Keep the dropdown trigger and accessible clear button as sibling buttons, putting the X inside the same surface without nesting interactive controls. Cap the desktop width at 16rem and tighten the selected shared rule target's segment padding so long patterns truncate sooner.
- 2026-08-12 Flow control mobile tabs and toolbar: Keep the real two-tab strip visible horizontally on mobile via `ContentPanelTabs.mobileMode = 'tabs'`; preserve the existing dropdown default for other panels. Hide Limit counters text search below `sm`. Render the rule selector as one compact secondary control with one leading Filters icon, quiet `Rule is`, its value/shared iconless rule target, and one trailing chevron.
- 2026-08-12 collapsed Flow control navigation: `LimitsSidebarItem` links its parent to Rules, but its parent `match` must cover the `/flow-control` section boundary (`pathname === path || pathname.startsWith(path + '/')`). This highlights the collapsed parent on Rules, Limit counters, and future child pages while expanded navigation continues to highlight the matching subitem.
- 2026-08-12 tab toolbar alignment: `ContentPanel`'s tab toolbar slot is 44px tall while active toolbar content is normally 36px. Use `items-end` on the shared slot so toolbars sit 4px lower and align optically with the tabs while retaining each toolbar's existing small bottom inset.
- 2026-08-12 gauge tab icon fill: `ContentPanel` tabs apply a generic fill to child SVGs. Lucide Gauge renders the needle first and the open arc second; filling the arc closes its shape and paints over the needle. Opt the Gauge tab icon out with `!fill-none` rather than changing the shared animated counter gauge.
- 2026-08-12 Flow control tab implementation verification: A shared `FlowControlPage` hero plus `ContentPanel tabs={flowControlTabs(baseUrl, selectedId)}` renders Rules/Limit counters as accessible route-backed tabs with the active route's toolbar in the trailing slot. Browser checks confirmed both path transitions, selected states, full-width and 640/560px layouts without horizontal document overflow; reset temporary viewport overrides after testing.
- 2026-08-12 route-backed Flow control tabs: `ContentPanel` already supports `tabs.items[].href`, controlled `selectedId`, and a trailing toolbar portal. Use it for Rules and Limit counters so `/flow-control/rules` and `/flow-control/counters` remain the canonical selected-tab state while each page supplies its own `ContentPanelToolbar`. Prefer explicit route segments over a permissive `:tab`, and use a shared **Flow control** hero to avoid repeating the selected tab name immediately above the tab.
- 2026-08-12 exact counter endpoint removal: Once the counter detail page is removed, delete `POST /query/limits/counter`, `getUserLimit`, its OpenAPI identity schema/generated operation, router binding, export, and focused tests. The list table's local `LimitCounterIdentity` remains a UI presentation shape and is unrelated.
- 2026-08-12 exact counter endpoint usage: After removing the counter detail page, no UI hook or component calls `POST /query/limits/counter`; `CounterTable`'s `LimitCounterIdentity` is a local presentation/navigation shape only. The endpoint now exists solely in query routing, OpenAPI/generated types, handler tests, and should be removed if keeping only current UI requirements.
- 2026-08-12 exact limit-counter lookup: `(scope, l1, l2)` is the concrete counter identity, so `getUserLimit` should query that identity directly without a redundant `LIMIT 1`, matching exact rule lookup semantics and avoiding silently masking duplicates.
- 2026-08-12 self on simplified-handler import residue: Removing cursor construction exposed an already-unnecessary `FilterItem` type import in `listUserLimits.ts`; focused ESLint caught it. Re-audit imports immediately after collapsing helper abstractions.
- 2026-08-12 self on multiline return patch: While simplifying rule sorting, I split `return` from its conditional expression, which would trigger automatic semicolon insertion and return `undefined`. Keep the expression on the return line or wrap it in parentheses, and inspect small helpers immediately after patching.
- 2026-08-12 limitPagination scope mismatch: The generic helper implements signed query-shape cursors and deterministic multi-column keyset predicates, but the active Rules and Limit counters hooks are single `useQuery` calls that never send `after`; they fetch at most 1,001 rows once and paginate 25 client-side. Unless the UI will fetch server pages past 1,000, retain only limit clamping plus sentinel-row `hasMore` and remove cursor/keyset machinery and its OpenAPI `after`/`nextCursor` contract.
- 2026-08-12 unused limit-target query cleanup: The removed counter-detail/VQueue surface left `/query/limits/targets` with no UI consumer. Remove its handler, router binding, OpenAPI operation/types, fixture data/query branch, and dedicated tests together so no dormant contract remains.
- 2026-08-12 self on repeated Nx graph stall: I ran `nx test query` even though the napkin already records that Nx graph construction hangs in this checkout; it produced no test output and had to be stopped. Prefer direct Vitest/TypeScript/ESLint commands for focused validation here.
- 2026-08-12 self on session-read command discipline: I combined the napkin skill and repository napkin reads with shell chaining and a separator despite the existing one-command-per-check rule. Use independent orchestrated reads from the start; do not add visual separators through shell commands.
- 2026-08-12 client pagination reset state: Do not serialize search/filter/sort fields into delimiter-based strings for `useLimitListPagination`. Accept the actual memoized server request object as reset state; in the counters route, build and reuse one `counterRequest` for both `useListUserLimits` and pagination. Rules likewise pass their memoized `ruleRequest`.
- 2026-08-12 effect-free client pagination normalization: Store the request object and observed page count with the selected page. During render, derive page 0 for a new request and clamp to the last valid page after collection shrinkage; use a guarded same-component state adjustment to persist that normalized value before commit. This avoids reset/synchronization effects, prevents a transient empty page, and prevents a later collection growth from restoring a stale page index.
- 2026-08-12 self on literal delimiter search: Searching for `\u0000` with a normal ripgrep regex made ripgrep interpret it as a NUL and reject the pattern. Use fixed-string mode with a single-quoted literal when auditing escaped source delimiters.
- 2026-08-12 Limit counter sorting contract: Support only `usage`, `pattern`, and `waiting` end to end. `pattern` means `rule_pattern`; default to `waiting DESC`. Limit counter identity and Limit remain visible but are not sortable. Keep separate OpenAPI schemas so Rules expose only pattern sorting and counters cannot send legacy aliases (`counter`, `limit`, `rule`, `waiters`, `concurrency_limit`, `rule_pattern`).
- 2026-08-12 self on orchestration syntax: I mistyped quoted object keys in a `functions.exec` JavaScript tool call, causing the inspection script to fail before any nested calls ran. Keep orchestration objects syntactically plain and re-read the call before sending.
- 2026-08-12 exact rule lookup: `sys_rules.pattern` is the rule identity; the rule book stores rules in `HashMap<RulePattern, PersistedRule>`, and Restate's CLI exact-pattern lookup does not use a limit. Do not add `LIMIT 1` to `WHERE pattern = ...`; use the first optional row only to map an empty result to 404.
- 2026-08-12 keyset predicate formatting: A single-column cursor predicate should render directly as `pattern > 'value'`, not `((pattern > 'value'))`. Preserve grouped `(a > x) OR (a = x AND b > y)` clauses only for multi-column keysets where boolean precedence matters.
- 2026-08-12 responsive Flow Control tables: Numeric `defaultWidth` values in React Aria remain pixel widths while the table contracts, so removing only `minWidth` is insufficient. Let `PanelTableColumn.defaultWidth` accept React Aria `fr` sizes, use proportional `fr` widths plus optional desktop `maxWidth` caps for flexible Rules/Limit-counter columns, and keep only true metadata/action widths fixed (especially Level at 96px). Intrinsic badges and identity chips must use `max-w-full min-w-0` and truncate their descriptive portion before overlap.
- 2026-08-12 self on inspection command chaining: I combined the Scope and LimitKey reads with `sed ... && sed ...`, repeating the no-separator inspection mistake. Use separate `exec_command` calls in parallel instead of shell control operators.
- 2026-08-12 rule counter-summary aggregation: The Rules page always restricts its statistics query with `rule_pattern IN (...)` using the patterns returned by the bounded rules query; this is unrelated to the Limit counters dropdown filter. Because grouping by an IN-list of N patterns can produce at most N rows, do not append a redundant `LIMIT N` after `GROUP BY`.
- 2026-08-12 shared rule-list query cost: `useListLimitRules` is used by both the Rules table and the Limit counters rule dropdown. The Rules table consumes `num_counters` and `num_counters_with_waiters`, but the dropdown only consumes pattern and limit; using the same always-enriched endpoint makes the dropdown pay for an unnecessary `sys_user_limits` aggregate. If simplifying, make statistics opt-in or provide a lightweight rules list.
- 2026-08-12 flow-control sortable columns: Rules supports sorting only by Pattern. Do not expose sorting on Limit, Description, Status, or Level, and keep the rules query sort implementation pattern-only. Limit counters retains its useful operational sorts but Level is display-only; derive it from `l1`/`l2` and do not emit the SQL level-ranking CASE expression.
- 2026-08-12 limit query default size: Rules, Limit counters, and related limit-query handlers default to 1,000 returned rows. Queries fetch one sentinel row to determine `hasMore`, so default SQL snapshots must end in `LIMIT 1001`, never the old `LIMIT 101`.
- 2026-08-12 Chip rendering simplification: `ChipGroup` now has one rendering path—the former efficient behavior. Keep transitions and filter-based brightness effects disabled unconditionally; do not reintroduce a rendering prop/type or a separate default path. `LimitRuleTarget` no longer passes rendering policy through.
- 2026-08-12 review-marker workflow: When the user says they have put `// review:` comments, search for and address those existing markers; do not add new review notes. In this pass, the active Rules and Limit counters collections are intentionally single bounded `useQuery` calls for up to 1,000 rows followed by 25-row client pagination. Remove unused detail-era hooks and routes rather than preserving infinite-query scaffolding.
- 2026-08-12 self on requested comment syntax: The user explicitly requested `// review:` comments, but I initially put one review note inside a SQL template as `-- review:`. Keep requested marker syntax in TypeScript source outside embedded SQL, even when the note refers to the query.
- 2026-08-11 simple Limit counter search SQL: Plain text searches only `scope`, `l1`, or `l2` with three simple OR clauses. If the input contains `/`, split it positionally and apply `scope AND l1 AND l2` substring filters. Do not concatenate SQL columns or search `rule_pattern`/`level`; the rule dropdown owns rule filtering.
- 2026-08-11 Flow Control hero spacing: Rules and Limit counters share `FlowControlHero`; keep its generous top breathing room but reduce bottom padding from `pb-12/md:pb-16` to `pb-4/md:pb-6` so the toolbar and table connect more closely to the page introduction.
- 2026-08-11 self on multi-file patches: I put a napkin hunk under the preceding source file without a new `Update File` header, so the patch failed atomically. Always introduce each file explicitly.
- 2026-08-11 rule status mutations: Enable/disable from the Rules row menu must open a confirmation dialog. On submit, cancel the active Rules request, snapshot its infinite-query data, optimistically update the matching rule's `disabled` field, roll back on error, and rely on the mutation hook's successful active refetch to reconcile version/stats.
- 2026-08-11 self on output truncation command: I piped an `rg` query into `head` while inspecting Admin API query-key construction, repeating the no-pipeline mistake. Narrow the `rg` glob/path instead.
- 2026-08-11 rule-mutation refetch metadata: Limit infinite queries constructed a fresh `useInfiniteQuery` options object but omitted `queryOptions.meta`, so `isQueryForPath` could not recognize `/query/limits/rules` and rule mutations did not reliably refetch it. Forward Admin API metadata on every limit infinite query and await active invalidation/refetch in every rule mutation success handler.
- 2026-08-11 Rules toolbar layout: Keep the secondary `New rule` action on the far left with `mr-auto ml-2`; leave refresh on the far right.
- 2026-08-11 Flow Control page descriptions: Keep hero copy user-facing and future-proof. Rules: `Configure flow-control policies for matching scopes and limit keys at any level of the hierarchy.` Limit counters: `Inspect limit counters across scopes and hierarchical limit keys, including their current usage and capacity.` Avoid `runtime`, `concrete`, and VQueue implementation terminology in these page-level descriptions.
- 2026-08-11 counter-detail removal: For now, Limit counters are list-only. Remove the `flow-control/counters/:scope` route, route module, detail feature/export, detail breadcrumb, counter href construction, CounterTable row action, and href passed to `LimitCounterTarget`. Keep rule-chip links to the rule-filtered counters list and retain the exact-counter backend API for possible future reuse.
- 2026-08-11 self on file discovery sorting: I piped `rg --files` through `sort` while auditing counter-detail files, violating the no-pipeline inspection rule. Use the natural `rg --files` output or sort mentally.
- 2026-08-11 self on Tailwind class assertions: A substring assertion for absence of `w-full` falsely matched `max-w-full`. Split class attributes on whitespace when asserting exact utility presence or absence.
- 2026-08-11 counter-table rule click target: `LimitRuleTarget`'s table variant is full-width for truncation, which makes its overlaid rule link fill the Rule cell even when the visible chips are narrow. In `CounterTable`, override the target root with `w-fit` so only the rendered rule chips navigate to the filtered counters page; retain the table chevron/presentation.
- 2026-08-11 default Limit counters filter: An absent `rule` query parameter means `Rule is any`, i.e. only counters governed by some configured rule. Keep `rule=all` as the explicit `Rule is any or none` state for including ungoverned/unlimited counters, and default generated counter-list links to `any` as well.
- 2026-08-11 consistent rule-filter trigger surfaces: Specific-rule selections intentionally use a transparent icon-variant Button around the shared rule target, so generic selections must not fall back to a full secondary-button pill. Use the same transparent wrapper and render `any` / `any or none` inside a compact shared Chip with the Filters icon.
- 2026-08-11 rule-filter trigger semantics: A bare rule target in the Limit counters toolbar does not communicate that it is the active dataset filter. Use the consistent visible grammar `Rule is [specific target]`, `Rule is any`, and `Rule is any or none`; the last state accurately explains that the unfiltered view also contains counters without a rule.
- 2026-08-11 compact waiting-counter signal: The relative waiter bar consumed about 230px and became a binary full/empty visualization when most counts were 0 or 1. Keep the sortable operational metric, label it `Waiting`, render zero as muted text and positive counts as compact amber badges, and use a roughly 120px column.
- 2026-08-11 self on result truncation: I piped an `rg` search into `head` while looking for warning-badge examples, repeating the no-pipeline mistake. Bound `rg` with a narrower path or accept its tool output cap.
- 2026-08-11 Limit column sizing: The Limit counters table's 130px minimum clipped the shared `concurrency = N` badge once values reached three digits. Match the Rules table with a 160px default and 150px minimum so common larger limits remain fully visible.
- 2026-08-11 self on scoped file discovery: I piped `rg --files` into another `rg` while locating the limits-route configs, repeating the established no-pipeline mistake. Use `rg --files` with explicit scoped globs such as `-g 'vite.config.ts' -g 'tsconfig.lib.json'`.
- 2026-08-11 flow-control table row treatment: Rules should match Limit counters with navigable row actions but no pointer cursor. Use the simple static `transition-none [content-visibility:auto]` row class; navigation does not require cursor styling or a per-row callback.
- 2026-08-11 concentric filterless chip radii: Once the filterless angled chip uses a 1px clipped outer border, the inset white surface cannot reuse the outer radius or its curves visibly diverge from the gray outline. Define separate `--chip-clip` and `--chip-inner-clip` paths, with `--chip-inner-radius: calc(var(--chip-radius) - 1px)`. Overriding only a radius variable inside an inherited `--chip-clip` does not work because that custom property resolves on the outer element.
- 2026-08-11 filterless chip clipping correction: A clipped border pseudo-element behind an unclipped `data-chip` wrapper still left the wrapper's normal rectangular `box-shadow` visible at angled edges; a red debug background exposed the mismatch. Clip the outer wrapper itself, make its zinc background the border, inset the root with `p-px`/`h-full`, and use only an inset shadow. This keeps every visible background/border layer on `--chip-clip` while retaining `filter: none`.
- 2026-08-11 reversible filterless chip-border experiment: At the user's request, the shared angled `Chip` border no longer uses chained `drop-shadow()` filters. Keep the experiment isolated in `SHAPED_BORDER` and `SHAPED_SEAM`: the clipped outer zinc surface and inset root form the outline, an inset shadow supplies restrained depth, and a clipped 4px seam ribbon replaces the nested-segment drop shadow. Browser computed styles confirmed `filter: none` for outer chips and L1/L2 seams. This is intentionally easy to revert if visual or performance testing is unfavorable.
- 2026-08-11 temporary counter-row cursor probe: At the user's request, `CounterTable` temporarily passes the static `rowStyles()` string instead of a per-row clickable variant to test whether the cursor-class callback contributes to hover/scroll lag. Row actions remain enabled; restore the href-based pointer treatment after the performance comparison unless the user chooses otherwise.
- 2026-08-11 rule-filter identity after icon integration: Render specific-rule dropdown options and the selected exact-rule trigger with the complete shared `LimitRuleTarget`, including its sliders icon inside Scope and standard chip rendering. Do not keep the earlier iconless-menu exception or efficient border; the integrated icon no longer creates a repetitive detached tile.
- 2026-08-11 target table rendering correction: Do not automatically switch `LimitRuleTarget` or `LimitCounterTarget` to `ChipGroup` efficient rendering in tables. `ServiceTarget` uses the standard renderer in denser tables and keeps the intended zinc border/shadow; earlier limit-table lag was not caused by that border. Preserve efficient rendering only as an explicit caller opt-in.
- 2026-08-11 limit-target visual identity: Match `ServiceTarget` by embedding the Gauge/Filters icon inside the meaningful Scope segment rather than creating a detached icon-only chip. Use a restrained `h-3.5` zinc-400 icon, preserve amber only on an at-capacity gauge, and let the native zero-gap `ChipGroup` form one continuous identity.
- 2026-08-11 limit-counter toolbar grouping: Place the rule selector at the far left as the table's dataset/scope control. Keep search and refresh grouped at the far right; the filter trigger can use `order-first mr-auto` without changing the dropdown behavior or responsive search sizing.
- 2026-08-11 target chip optical spacing: For shared rule and limit-counter identities, a Scope segment following the angled icon connector needs `pl-2` rather than the standalone `pl-1`; straight Scope chips retain the denser inset. Keep the leading Gauge/Sliders segment subtly distinct with `bg-zinc-50`, including the at-capacity Gauge state, instead of pure white.
- 2026-08-11 latency probe complete: The two-second Rules/Limit counters query delay was only for visual diagnosis and has been removed. Retain the loading-aware `bodyKey`, `isFetching` dependencies, skeleton state, and fresh counter-query cache policy as the production fix.
- 2026-08-11 loading collection remount: Follow Invocations by giving each `PanelTable` a body key derived from its visible row IDs. For live Rules/Limit counters, prefix that key with `loading`/`ready`; refreshes retain the previous data object, so item IDs alone do not change, while the loading prefix forces React Aria to replace the visibly cached collection with skeleton rows.
- 2026-08-11 temporary limit-query latency probe: To visually validate `isFetching` skeletons, hold both Rules and Limit counters query promises open for two seconds after the API response. Keep the artificial delay isolated behind the clearly named `LIMIT_QUERY_ARTIFICIAL_DELAY_MS` helper and remove it after the user finishes inspection.
- 2026-08-11 React Aria table loading dependencies: Passing `isFetching` only as `isLoading` may not invalidate the cached table collection body at the route boundary. Include the query's `isFetching` value in `CounterTable.dependencies` / `PanelTable.bodyDependencies`, use it as the table loading state on refresh and query-key transitions, and hide pagination until the fresh body is ready. Apply the same pattern to Rules and Limit counters.
- 2026-08-11 self on repeated hook patch context: I used a generic `useInfiniteQuery` hunk and initially applied live counter cache options to `useListLimitRules`, the preceding near-identical hook. Anchor repeated hook edits with the exported function name and inspect the exact block immediately after patching.
- 2026-08-11 live limit-counter filters: The app-wide QueryClient treats data as fresh for five minutes and disables mount refetches. Operational `sys_user_limits` collections must override this with zero stale/cache retention and an always-on mount refetch, otherwise revisiting a rule selection silently restores stale rows and stale pagination. While the fresh request is in flight, render table skeletons from `isFetching` and suppress the cached pagination footer.
- 2026-08-11 limit-counter pagination discoverability: Exact-rule filtering runs before the 25-row client slice, so zero-waiter counters that live on a later page of the Any-rule view move onto page 1 when narrowed. A footer placed after row 25 can sit just below the viewport and make those rows look missing. Keep pagination persistently discoverable, and ensure server `hasMore` can drive loading even when the currently loaded client page count is one.
- 2026-08-11 self on browser event tracing: I assumed `playwright.waitForEvent('response')` supported network events; this browser surface only supports `download` and `filechooser`, and the uncaught rejection reset the kernel. Do not use it for request tracing. Use server-side query probes or product instrumentation, and wrap uncertain async browser calls so a rejection cannot terminate the session.
- 2026-08-11 self on inspection command chaining: Right after correcting a pipeline mistake, I combined two independent `sed` reads with `&&`. Keep every inspection as a separate tool call; adjacent read-only files are not a reason to use a shell control operator.
- 2026-08-11 self on repeated file-list filtering: I piped `rg --files` into `sed` while locating Vitest configs despite the established no-pipeline rule. Use `rg --files` with scoped globs and rely on the tool output limit; never add a shell pipeline just to shorten discovery output.
- 2026-08-11 selected rule trigger: Do not invent a second inline rule identity. Render the full shared `LimitRuleTarget` with its leading sliders chip, but use an icon-variant Button as a transparent behavior/focus wrapper and keep only the dropdown chevrons outside the component. Plain All/Any selections retain the compact secondary button.
- 2026-08-11 rule-filter option metadata: In the Limit counters rule dropdown, keep the selected trigger identity-only, but show each specific rule's configured `LimitValue` right-aligned in its menu option. Use the full rule objects already returned by `useListLimitRules`, including the disabled treatment, instead of reducing the dropdown data to pattern strings.
- 2026-08-11 self on DropdownItem test harness: `DropdownItem` always invokes `useHrefWithQueryParams`, even for a non-navigation item, so isolated tests need a `MemoryRouter`. Include the router wrapper in the first test rather than misreading the failure as a component regression.
- 2026-08-11 approved rule-filter selector: Use the shared `LimitRuleTarget` treatment in Limit counters and keep one integrated sliders icon per exact rule. Chip filters/drop-shadows inside `DropdownItem` need an opt-in overflow-visible content boundary; the default `truncate` wrapper otherwise clips their outer border and shadow.
- 2026-08-11 rule-filter native surfaces: When the selector uses shared `LimitRuleTarget`, do not override the Scope or Limit Key backgrounds. Preserve the component's native surface hierarchy and vary only the contextual leading icon visibility.
- 2026-08-11 rule-filter preview simplification: Prefer the shared `LimitRuleTarget` for selector options and the selected exact rule; after integrating the icon into Scope, render the full target instead of maintaining a separate iconless dropdown variant.
- 2026-08-11 rule-filter design preview: When the user is unconvinced by a proposed selector treatment, create an isolated temporary route that uses the real toolbar/dropdown primitives and representative Scope/L1/L2 patterns. Keep the live Limit counters selector untouched until the preview is approved.
- 2026-08-11 self on browser API discovery: I searched the minified `browser-client.mjs` for navigation methods, producing a huge truncated result, when the runtime tab already exposed `goto()` directly. Inspect the bound tab prototype or use the previously documented `tab.goto(url)` method; do not search bundled minified browser code.
- 2026-08-11 self on cross-file patch placement: I placed a `LimitTargets.spec.tsx` assertion inside the `LimitRuleTarget.tsx` file hunk, so the atomic patch failed verification. Keep each changed file's hunk explicitly under its own `Update File` header, even when the edits implement one small shared behavior.
- 2026-08-11 Flow-control rule filter identity: In Limit counters rule selectors, do not expose slash-delimited rule patterns as the primary visual model or use `scope/l1/l2` search examples. Render exact choices and the selected value with the shared `LimitRuleTarget` chips (Scope plus hierarchical Limit Key); keep the encoded raw pattern only in URL/query state. For large dropdown lists, use tooltip-free efficient chip rendering.

- 2026-08-10 DataFusion utilization sort: `sys_user_limits.usage` is always populated and a configured concurrency limit originates as `NonZeroU32`; only `NULL` represents unlimited. Do not generate zero-limit branches or a `1e308` sentinel. The repeated `concurrency_limit IS NULL` condition across sort keys was hoisted to `__common_expr_1` and triggered DataFusion `SanityCheckPlan` in partitioned Top-K. Use `(concurrency_limit IS NULL)` as one boolean key, `COALESCE(CAST(usage AS DOUBLE) / concurrency_limit, 0)` as the ratio, and the unique `(scope,l1,l2)` identity only.
- 2026-08-10 self: Updating full inline SQL snapshots exposed whitespace-only lines emitted by conditional interpolations in the production query strings; Prettier cannot remove whitespace inside string literals and `git diff --check` correctly flagged the snapshots. Build optional `WHERE` clauses with their leading newline and append them directly to `FROM ...` so absent clauses emit no blank padded line.
- 2026-08-10 self: After converting the Rules and Limit counters list endpoints from GET to paginated POST, I initially left `isLimitsQuery` mutation invalidation matching their old GET methods and omitted the new exact-counter endpoint. Whenever an endpoint method/path changes, audit React Query invalidation predicates as part of the same change so create/edit/delete refresh every paged and detail cache.
- 2026-08-10 Flow-control mock drill-down QA: The mock rule store is mutable across UI actions, so manually revisiting a previously created pattern can produce stale-looking detail results. Verify rule/counter consistency by following the current global counter row's actual rule href; `hot-object/dense/*` correctly returned its three counters after the pagination changes.
- 2026-08-10 self: While investigating an empty paged rule-detail fixture, I chained two `sed` reads with `&&`, repeating the repository's no-separator violation. Use separate read calls or one read with multiple `-e` ranges; never combine inspection commands with shell control operators.
- 2026-08-10 self: A broad pagination UI patch malformed the Rules page's mapped-row callback with a duplicated `};`, which Prettier caught. After multi-file structural patches, format the changed UI files before launching typechecks and fix syntax in isolation.
- 2026-08-10 Flow-control query safety: Every list query must use bounded keyset pagination with a default page of 100, maximum 500, and `limit + 1` to derive `hasMore`; sorting/filtering belongs server-side. Detail pages use an exact identity query with `LIMIT 1`. Rule counter statistics must be restricted to patterns in the already-bounded rule page rather than globally grouping every counter. Existing fixed 100-row results need cursors, not silent truncation.
- 2026-08-10 self: While verifying Limit counters pagination, I guessed `libs/data-access/admin-api/src/lib/api/query.json`; this repository keeps the query API schema elsewhere. Use `rg --files` before reading a schema path instead of carrying an assumed layout from another data-access library.
- 2026-08-10 Flow-control target identity: Keep `LimitCounterTarget` and `LimitRuleTarget` as the reusable exported identity components. In compact/default/table contexts, lead the Scope chip with Gauge for a limit counter and Filters/SlidersHorizontal for a rule; avoid duplicating the large icon already supplied by the detail-page Header, while allowing callers to opt the icon back in.
- 2026-08-10 self: While locating Gauge/Filters icon names, I piped `rg` into `head` despite the established search rule. Use a bounded `rg` path/glob and the tool output limit; never add a truncation pipeline.

## Skill Notes

- 2026-08-10 admin API generation formatting: Running `openapi-typescript` directly rewrites the generated declaration and merged JSON without the repository formatting step, creating thousands of noisy line changes. If the prescribed Nx `create` target stalls, run `merge.mjs`, `openapi-typescript`, then Prettier on `index.d.ts` and `output.json` explicitly.

- 2026-08-10 self on stalled admin API generator: I retried `pnpm nx create admin-api` even though this checkout has repeated silent Nx graph stalls; it produced no target output for over a minute and needed interruption. Inspect the target's concrete commands and execute them directly when the same graph stall pattern recurs.

- 2026-08-10 self on parsing large command output: I attempted `JSON.parse` on a large `git show` result, but the execution layer injected a truncation warning into the output and invalidated the JSON. Use narrow `git grep`/`git blame -L` queries or parse only output known to be below the truncation limit.

- 2026-08-10 Restate skill cache path: The session catalog pointed at `restatedev/0.0.13`, but the installed cache had moved to `restatedev/0.0.14`. Locate the current `SKILL.md` with a scoped `rg --files` when the advertised versioned path is missing.

- 2026-08-10 Flow Control rule/counter detail foundation: Current main already exposes `useGetLimitRule` and the filtered/sorted `useListLimitCounters` endpoint, but the UI route only registers `/flow-control/rules`. The old `origin/NN_vqueque` prototype's counter drill-down depended on `useListLimitTargets`/`LimitTargetRow`, which are not present on current main; treat that operational VQueue query as a separate API slice to redesign or port rather than assuming the old tab UI is ready to restore.

- 2026-08-10 self on command hygiene relapse: While inspecting the limits-detail task, I chained `git status` and `rg` with `&&` despite the repo's established no-separator rule. Keep status and repository searches as independent tool calls, even when both are read-only.

- 2026-08-10 Flow Control counter-ratio label: A bare `1 / 2` is not self-explanatory, and `Affected` is too vague. Keep rows compact but label the single-line column `Backlogged / total counters`; define a backlogged counter in the dark tooltip as one with at least one VQueue waiting for capacity. Give the column enough minimum width that this explanatory header remains visible.

- 2026-08-10 Flow Control delete form: The rule deletion confirmation already uses shared React Router `Form` and `SubmitButton`; provide `formAction="/limits/rules/bulk-delete"` and `formMethod="POST"` so automatic mutation-pending detection matches `useDeleteLimitRule`. Keep `isPending` only to prevent cancellation during the mutation, and label the destructive action `Delete`, not `Delete rule`.

- 2026-08-10 Flow Control refresh styling: Use the shared `icon` button variant for the rules-table refresh action; keep it unboxed with no white background rather than styling it as a secondary button.

- 2026-08-10 Flow Control form and tests: Keep focused coverage on rule-pattern matching semantics and the interactive `RulePatternBuilder` hierarchy. `KeyPill` must use the shared React Aria-backed `Button`, not a native button. Mutation dialogs should use React Router `Form` with an action equal to the React Query mutation path (create/update rules: `/limits/rules`) and the shared `SubmitButton`, allowing it to detect mutation pending state automatically instead of receiving manual pending state.

- 2026-08-10 Flow Control rule timestamps: The rules-list UI does not consume `last_modified_millis_since_epoch`, so `LimitRuleWithStats` is an explicit list-item schema without that field and the list SQL must not select `last_modified`. Keep the timestamp in the server `RuleResponse` contract for single-rule/create/update responses; select the native ISO `last_modified` for the single-rule query and convert it with `Date.parse` in the TypeScript adapter instead of casting in SQL.

- 2026-08-10 Flow Control rule-list queries: Fetch configured `sys_rules` rows and aggregated live `sys_user_limits` counter summaries as two backend queries started with `Promise.all`, then merge by `rule_pattern` with zero defaults. Keep this behind one browser request so live statistics do not cause a second table update or reorder. Mock `/query` must recognize the grouped counter SQL and return aggregate rows rather than raw counters.

- 2026-08-10 Flow Control rules refresh: Put a compact secondary icon-only refresh action immediately before the primary `New rule` toolbar button. Refetch only the rules query, prevent duplicate fetches while it is active, rotate the refresh glyph as feedback, and retain a tooltip plus accessible state-aware label.

- 2026-08-10 self on multiline `rg` search: I embedded `\n` in a normal ripgrep pattern while looking for accessible icon buttons, which ripgrep rejects without multiline mode. Search the single-line attribute directly or run separate scoped searches; do not introduce multiline mode for simple JSX discovery.

- 2026-08-10 Flow control rule pressure summary: On the rules list, do not expose the aggregate number of waiting VQueues. Show a `Counters` ratio instead: concrete counters with at least one waiting VQueue divided by all concrete counters governed by the rule. Normalize the amber bar per row to that ratio, keep the exact `affected / total` values visible, and explain the encoding through the shared dark inline tooltip. Reserve raw VQueue counts for a future concrete-counter detail surface.

Inspect existing Nx processes/locks or run the declared Vite command directly before retrying another Nx wrapper.

- 2026-08-10 self on query test validation: I invoked the Nx test wrapper for the focused `query` handler test even though the napkin records silent Nx graph stalls. It yielded only a live session and needed interruption; use the project's direct Vitest config for focused query tests.

- 2026-08-10 self on project discovery relapse: I included nonexistent root `project.json` in a scoped `rg` path list while locating the admin API generator, despite the existing note. Search only confirmed paths such as `package.json`, `nx.json`, and `libs/**/project.json`.

- 2026-08-10 self on visualization renderer path: I resolved `scripts/render.py` against the plugin package root instead of the directory containing `skills/visualize/SKILL.md`. Skill-relative assets live under `skills/visualize/`; resolve referenced paths from the selected SKILL file as instructed.

- 2026-08-10 `sys_rules.last_modified` conversion: The query JSON renders the raw `TimestampMillisecond` as an ISO string, while `RuleResponse` requires numeric `last_modified_millis_since_epoch`. In the current DataFusion query engine, `to_unixtime(timestamp) * 1000` truncates the millisecond fraction (`...46.553Z` becomes `...46000`); use `CAST(last_modified AS BIGINT)` to preserve the underlying epoch milliseconds. A `LEFT JOIN` to aggregated rule waiters still needs `COALESCE(..., 0)` because unmatched rules otherwise produce a null/omitted `num_waiters` despite the response contract requiring a number.

- 2026-08-10 self on direct `/query` probing: Posting SQL with only `content-type: application/json` returns Arrow IPC bytes. Include `accept: application/json` when inspecting query rows in the terminal.

- 2026-08-10 Flow control URL: The UI route prefix is `/flow-control`, with the rules page at `/flow-control/rules`. Keep the Restate Admin API endpoints under `/limits`; those are server contracts, not web navigation paths.

- 2026-08-07 self on repeated file-discovery pipeline: I piped `rg --files` into `rg` while locating icon/sidebar project configs despite the established no-pipeline rule. Use direct `rg --files -g 'tsconfig.lib.json' -g 'project.json' <roots>` instead.

- 2026-08-07 Flow control navigation identity: Use the shared `Filters` icon name mapped to Lucide `SlidersHorizontal` for Flow control across sidebar, page, dialog, and empty states. Treat Flow control as a sidebar parent and show `Rules` as its nested navigation item.

- 2026-08-07 self on multi-file patch context: The first route scaffold patch assumed an `invocation-ui` alias existed next to `overview-route`, so the whole atomic patch failed verification.
